- Owner (comment): score selection model (click section = select moment; click/Shift-click/rectangle select dots; drag moves selection; empty click = playhead; no click-to-play). PLAN §11.3 rev 7 "Score selection". Combined with grouped note changes as M4.5-23, after M4.5-22. Doubled-voice marks = M4.5-24.
- Owner: split prefers octaves over unisons; all UI text says "notes" instead of "voices". PLAN rev 7. Assigned with doubled-voice marks as M4.5-24.

## M4.5-21: lane stacking; Add vs Next (app, 2026-11-21)
- **Stacking** (`stackRows` in `editor/lane.ts`, replaces `packRows`/`packBars` and the 2-row cap): greedy interval packing in px, by left edge, ties in piece order; an item's box = marker + label ∪ its span (`itemBoxPx`), so overlapping spans or labels never share a sub-row. A moment uses the same sub-row index in Moments and Transitions (bar stays under its marker). Rows ≥ 2 (old height when nothing stacks); Lane.tsx sets `--mom-h`/`--trans-h` and `data-subrows` on the track, lane.css derives row tops, Level top and track height. Repacked on zoom/item change, frozen during a drag (as before). Height changes with zoom when labels start to collide.
- **Add** = exactly the playhead (`playheadTime()` in shortcuts.ts, 1 µs rounding): palette click, letters, palette effects and palette hover preview. No bar-grid snap (rev 7). Palette drops (`palette-drop.ts`) still snap to the grid: left to M4.5-22's snapping work.
- **Next** (`editor/suggestions.ts`): `suggestionTime(piece, score)` = latest end of any unmuted moment (span end, walk `until`, level `at + over`), 0 with none; no grid ceil, independent of playhead and selection. `suggestionsAt` reads the state just before an explicit `end.at` when that comes first and shifts the moments to t. `appendMoment` inserts and moves an explicit `end.at` out to the moment's estimated span end + 4 s when it would leave less; automatic ends are left alone. Used for add (one undo entry) and the hover preview (`startMomentPreview` gains a `build` arg). Poll recomputes only when `compiled` changes (no longer paused while playing).
- **UI:** two groups, "Add at playhead" (solid frame, solid buttons) and "Next at end m:ss.s" (tinted dashed accent box, pill buttons); toolbar aria-labels say where each adds.
- **Tests:** unit `lane.test.ts` (stackRows: uncapped, span overlap, stable ties, no overlap on a row), `shortcuts.test.ts` (Add not snapped with a grid), new `suggestions.test.ts` (end time ignores playhead/selection/muted, append + undo label, end extension, state before an early end). e2e: `suggestions.spec` rewritten (end time, append, end extension, preview from the end), `palette.spec` (+ no grid snap, separate styled groups), `lane-rows.spec` (+ three overlapping moments on three sub-rows, lane taller, bars under markers inside Transitions, click hits the third row).
- Owner (comment): past and future lines look equally dim. Causes: the future is not dimmed before the first play (`idle` ghost = 1, loop.ts), and a selection dims every unselected line by DIM_ALPHA on both sides. Fix queued in M4.5-25 (with doubled marks and the notes rename): in the editor the future is always dimmed, and the selection keeps the past/future contrast.
- Owner (comment): canvas should not stop at the piece length. PLAN rev 7 "Open-ended canvas". Queued M4.5-26 after M4.5-22.


## M4.5-22: snapping to note changes, bar lines, drag edge (app + render, 2026-11-21)
- **Bars** (`render/bars.ts` `barGrid`): beat = 60/`gridBpm`, bar = beat·`view.meter`; beats drawn only ≥ 8 px apart, bars thinned ×2 until ≥ 24 px. Score: `ScoreStyle.bars` (editor layout only, from loop.ts), beats `rule` 0.6, bars `ink2` 0.3, behind the lines, no allocation. Ruler: two CSS-gradient strips (`ruler-beats`, `ruler-bars`, `data-step`).
  - **Core (additive):** `ViewSettings.meter?` (int 1..32, default 4); schema + file-format regenerated. `gridBpm` doc now "tempo of the drawn bar grid".
  - **Choice:** with no `gridBpm`, bars are drawn at 60 bpm 4/4 (bar = 4 s), so every piece shows bars (owner asked for bars in the view; there was no tempo UI). Inspector, nothing selected: **Bars** fieldset, Tempo (`insp-bpm`) and Beats per bar (`insp-meter`), undoable.
- **Snap** (`editor/snap.ts`, pure): `changePoints(score, piece)` = both ends of every moving segment (p0 ≠ p1: arrivals, walk steps, note points) + each unmuted moment's `at`, sorted, cached per score. `snapTime(t, {points, exclude, prefer}, pps, alt)`: nearest preferred time within 8 px, else nearest point within 8 px, else free (ms); Alt free. Points are taken at gesture start; exclusions `ofVoice(moment, voice)` (dot drags), `ofMoments(ids)` (lane drags, walk steps). `dotSnaps`: a walk step prefers its walk's other steps' arrivals, falling back to points outside that walk.
  - Used by dot drags (ArrivalDots), double-click/N-click adds (canvas-input), lane moment drags (Lane.tsx: only the snap call + a `snaps` field on Drag), palette drops. **Bar grid no longer snaps:** Add uses M4.5-21's exact `playheadTime()`. Left on the grid: lane arrow nudges (step = grid beat, else 0.1 s), ruler loop edges, Suggestions (other builder).
- **Drag edge jump, cause:** the view auto-fits its pitch range to the score (`pitchExtent`). Dragging below the lowest line lowered the target, the view re-fitted lower, the same pointer y then meant a lower pitch (runaway), until the pitch left the voice range and the compiler folded it up an octave (§7.8): the jump. Fix: during a dot or note-point drag `camera.pitch` holds the view's range (loop passes it as `CameraState.pitchRange`); pointer y is clamped to the plot area (`editor/drag-edge.ts` `edgePitch`); `dragArrival` clamps every kind's pitch (chord, walk, note, override) to the dot's voice range (unknown voice: the pool's union), `inRange`, and note-point adds clamp to the hit voice's range.
  - **Gap:** a note dot with no known voice is clamped to the pool's union range, so a narrower voice range could still fold.
- **Tests:** unit `snap.test.ts` (px threshold vs zoom, Alt, exclusion, prefer, change points, walk `dotSnaps`, edge/range clamps), `arrival-drag.test.ts` (out-of-range drags clamp, no fold), `ruler.test.ts` (`barGrid`), `palette-drop.test.ts` (snaps to a change, not the grid), `shortcuts.test.ts` (no grid snap at the playhead). e2e `snap.spec.ts`: split dot dropped 0.1 s from the shift's start snaps at fit zoom, stays free at a 6 s window; walk step moved a few px keeps integer ticks; dragging below the view: ghost moves down then holds at the edge, pitch lower and in range, dot lands there; ruler bars/beats steps, a bar pixel on the canvas, tempo/meter fields change them. Updated for the no-grid behaviour: lane.spec (drag lands at 3.8, not 4), note-points (double-click at 10.2 stays free), editor-polish (palette drop near 6.1 snaps to the unison at 6).
- Owner (comment): stacked (same-pitch) voices moving together should not diverge by default; toggle. PLAN rev 7 "Stacked voices move as one" (`glide.splitStacked`). Added to M4.5-24 (core).
## M4.5-24: octaves before unisons (core, 2026-11-21)
**Cause confirmed:** a split's `pitches` list shorter than its voices doubled exactly (lanterns' 8-note split raised to 10 → 8 lines). `chord`/`degrees`/`current` notes already list every octave in the register as candidates, so they only double when the register holds too few.

**Rule** (`voicing.ts` `fillOctaves`, enabled by `voiceNotes({ octaves: true })` from split and chord `count` births): when the voicing returns fewer than k targets, each missing one takes a free octave of a target inside the register (above a slash bass), avoiding every target (also folded) and the destinations of untargeted active voices. Extra j prefers the note the old doubling picked (`evenIndices`, cyclic past the note count); among all free octaves it takes: fewest octaves from its note, then its note nearest the preferred one by index, then nearest the preferred pitch, then lower. It stops when no octave is free; `voiceWithBirths` then doubles as before. Distinct count = min(voices, octaves of the notes in range).
- **Decisions:** applies to every voicing and NoteSet kind (no-op where candidates already cover the register). It only adds targets the voicing could not supply: `nearest`'s per-voice doublings (minimal motion) and duplicates written in `pitches` stay. Range = the voicing register (union of the voiced voices' ranges, or a chord's explicit `register`, which the octaves respect). The fill runs before assignment, so in a split or count chord with fewer notes than existing voices the existing voices take octaves too. Birth parents (`orderParent`, M4.5-17), ordering and planning unchanged.
- **Not changed:** plain `chord` without `count`, and count chords that only lose voices (`voiceChord`, surjective DP): §6.2 says they double, and the owner's words cover splits. Same rule there would be a one-line change if wanted.
- **no-effect:** a split/count chord from a unison with one pitch now sounds octaves (an effect); the "only doubles the parents" cases in `no-effect.test.ts` and `chord-count.test.ts` now use a range narrower than an octave.
- **Goldens:** none changed (example splits list as many pitches as voices).
- **Tests:** `tests/unit/core/octaves-before-unisons.test.ts` (fillOctaves rules, voiceNotes flag, slash bass lowest, split 4→10 distinct + no line artifacts, narrow ranges 6 / 4 distinct, `current` unchanged, lanterns 8→10, count chord births + explicit register, plain chord still doubles, incremental == full); property `tests/property/core/octaves.property.test.ts` (voiceNotes and compiled split: distinct = min(voices, octaves in range), all octaves of the notes, no line artifacts).
- **Pre-existing at 1000 random runs** (fail identically with the fill disabled): compiler.property crossings (seed 512133634 path 31; −1185324365 path 662), ranges respected (811138243 path 954); chord-count "far side" (439040815 path 857). Spaced-pieces property times out (5 s) at 1000 runs.
- Owner (comment): the start becomes a moment, with a fresh piece = one-note chord at 0, and v1 `start` migrates (schema v2). M4.5-27, after M4.5-23 and the M4.5-24 follow-up, since both touch the schema. New > "Compose for me" shows the composer building live with live inputs: M4.5-28, after 27.
- Owner: lines never share a resting pitch. Merges keep a summed weight (loudness), unison stays its own moment, wobble goes. PLAN rev 8 "Distinct notes". M4.5-29 (core). The splitStacked follow-up is cancelled, and the doubled-voice marks are dropped from M4.5-25.
- The splitStacked follow-up was cancelled with nothing merged. Rare property failures to fix after M4.5-29, all with seeds, at 1000 runs:
  - `compiler.property`: crossings at 512133634/31 and -1185324365/662; ranges at 811138243/954; walk-over-point at 112518007/143.
  - chord-count "far side": 439040815/857.

## M4.5-26: open-ended canvas (app + render, 2026-11-21)
- **Camera** (`render/camera.ts`): `canvasEnd(D)` = max(4·D, D + 600 s). `clampWindow`/`clampStart`/`windowRange` (so `zoomAround`, slider, `cameraAt`, `cameraOf`, `panBy`) clamp to [0, canvasEnd] instead of the piece. A missing window fits the piece. `followStart`: a moved view (Smooth, Page turn) is still clamped to the piece (playback near the end unchanged); a playhead resting past the end is followed out there; an unmoved view (Off, Page between turns) is only clamped to the canvas, so a pan past the end stays. `fitWindow` sets start 0 + window D. ViewBar: Fit pressed only at exactly D; zoom-out disabled at the canvas max.
- **Seek (decision):** paused seeks/scrubs go anywhere on the canvas (so Add can land past the end); while playing, seeks clamp to the end; Play at or past the end restarts at 0 (existing rule).
- **End follows content** (`editor/end.ts`): automatic end already follows (compiler: last activity + 4 s). Explicit `end.at`: `followContent(before, after)` runs in `history.edit` and `updateGesture`, extending `end.at` to keep 4 s after any added/changed (by reference) unmuted moment (`momentReach`: span estimate, override arrivals, note `back`). Edits that change `end.at` themselves are left alone. `appendMoment` now calls the shared `extendEnd`. Covers Add, drops, lane/dot drags, note adds without touching `canvas-input.ts`, `ArrivalDots.tsx`, `actions.ts`, `Lane.tsx` (none edited).
  - Choice: editing any moment already cut off by an explicit end also extends the end to include it.
- **Adding past the end:** `defaultMoment` (new-moment.ts) and the palette effects (`effectTime`, palette-effects.ts) read the state just before the end when the add is at/after it (voices hold until a later end), else every type was greyed "no voice is sounding". Preview's default build extends the end too.
- **End handle** (Ruler.tsx, `.ruler-end`): line at the piece end (dashed when automatic), ruler past it hatched. Press within 6 px below the loop band = drag (gesture "Move the end", one undo entry, `withEnd`, snaps via `snapTime(changePoints)`, Alt free, min 0.25 s); no seek. Double-click = "Automatic end" (removes `end.at`, undoable).
- **Kept:** loop region clipped to the piece; player layout shares the open canvas (no grid there).
- **Tests:** unit `camera.test.ts` updated (canvas clamps, follow past the end, zoom past the end), new `tests/unit/app/end.test.ts` (reach, extend, followContent, withEnd, history/gesture + undo, store pan/zoom/fit/seek caps, palette past the end). e2e `open-canvas.spec.ts` (chromium): pan past the end shows bars + pan/zoom/Fit still work; ruler click past the end + Add extends `end.at`, undo; end handle drag (Alt) sets the end, undo, double-click automatic.
- **Full chromium e2e (dev + prod):** all pass after updating `app.spec` (slider's far end = the open canvas) and `view-follow.spec` (zoom-out enabled at Fit), except the timing gates `render.spec` 4 ms (7–8 ms here) and `app-perf` 60 fps, which fail the same way on the base commit 435e195 on this box.

## E2E: lane Escape + view-follow race (2026-11-21)
Cause: lane Escape test expected an exact mid-drag `at` of 5, but grid snapping was removed (M4.5-22), so Firefox/WebKit give 4.994; view-follow read the camera window right after a slider fill, before a frame had drawn.
Fix: mid-drag poll uses `toBeCloseTo(5, 1)` (final `toBe(2)` kept); the follow test polls `window` < 5 before reading it.

## M4.5-23: score selection, grouped note changes (core + app, 2026-11-21)
- **Core `note.changes?: { from, to }[]`** (types `NoteChange`; `from`/`to` now optional). A note has either `from`+`to` or a non-empty `changes` (schema refine rejects both/neither); file-format regenerated; older builds reject `changes` files. Read only through `noteChanges(m)`, written through `withNoteChanges(m, list)` (one change → `from`/`to`, several → `changes`), both in `moments/note.ts`. Compiler: each live change takes the nearest voice not taken by an earlier one (`nearestNote` gains `skip`); unordered: those voices move; `order`: one stable sort of the replaced set (`noteTargetsMany`). Unmatched changes warn `note-not-sounding` (the rest apply); all no-ops → `no-effect`. `MomentPlan.note` → `notes[]`; `NoteBack` holds `changes`, so `back` returns every change.
- **Selection** (`editor/dot-selection.ts`): selected dots are `ArrivalDot` snapshots, found among the selected moments' current dots by place (20 ms / ¼ st), else walk step or voice (`pickedIn`), so they survive recompiles and their own drags. Selecting dots selects their moments; ArrivalDots now shows the dots of every selected moment (one container per moment, old testids; picked dots `.picked`, `data-picked`). Dots of deselected moments are dropped (ArrivalDots effect; no store subscription, as unit mocks of `useEditor` lack `subscribe`).
- **Canvas** (`canvas-input.ts`, editor layout): click on a line → `sectionOwner` (segment's moment; a hold → the latest earlier move in that life; `editor/section.ts`) selected, Shift toggles it, dot selection cleared; click on empty background → seek, dot selection cleared, moment selection kept; drag past slop on background or line → marquee (`marquee.ts`, drawn by ArrivalDots per frame), selecting `dotsInRect` over `cachedArrivals` (Shift extends; an empty rectangle clears). Pan: wheel/trackpad, middle-button drag, Space-drag. **Choice:** in the editor Space toggles play on keyup unless it panned (player layout unchanged: immediate). Player layout keeps click-to-play and drag-pan. The M4.5-15 restore-transport workaround is gone. Escape clears dots too.
- **Drags** (`dragDots(dots, grabbed)` in actions.ts; `dragArrival` wraps it): `groupTargets` (same scale steps in the key at each dot, keeping a chromatic offset; Shift = same semitones; same dt, ≥ 0; voice ranges) → `moveDots` (`editor/group-move.ts`, one recipe per frame from the gesture start): chord notes (frozen once per chord) + `end` overrides, walk steps (all old notes removed first), other moments' overrides (never converted), note changes. Snap uses the grabbed dot, excluding every dragged dot's own points. One undo: "Move notes" (one dot: its old label). Moved dots stay selected at their targets.
- **Join/split** (`moveNoteChanges`, `noteHostAt`, JOIN_T = 0.1 ms): a note change's new `at` = its moment's `at` + dt. Landing on an unmuted note moment's `at` joins it; all changes of a moment moving together move the moment (same id); a subset splits into a new moment (src fields and effective seed, no overrides/label). Double-click/N-click adds join a note at that time (on a line it already changes: edits that change). **Deviation (§11.3 "Every dot is draggable"):** a note dot's sideways drag now moves the change's time (glide kept) instead of writing an `end` override, so join/split compare moment times.
- **UI:** lane label "Shift" for several changes (`momentText`); inspector one Note row per change (same testids, `stepNote(id, dir, change)`); ▲/▼ keys step every change of the selected note.
- **Tests:** unit `core/note-changes.test.ts` (forms, multi-voice, unison pair, order, back, partial match, no-effect, incremental == full, schema), `app/group-move.test.ts` (group math, marquee, identity, join, move, split, overrides, host, section owner, Shift label), `arrival-drag` (+ two adds → one Shift, dragDots one undo; note sideways moves `at`), canvas-input (Space on release), arrival-points (note `change`). Property arbitraries: note `more` → `changes` (1–2 extra). e2e `score-select.spec.ts` (marquee + group drag one undo, Shift-click/Escape, line click selects, empty click seeks without playing, two adds → Shift + split by drag, plain drag doesn't pan, Space-drag pans); app/view-follow pan specs use middle-button drag.
- **Traps:** dot buttons use `data-dot-moment` (`data-moment` selectors match lane items). Hover dots still carry `data-moment`.

## M4.5-29: distinct notes with weights (core + dsp, 2026-11-21) — WIP, handoff
PLAN rev 8 "Distinct notes". Builder stopped at ~200k context; committed with `pnpm test`, `test:perf`, lint, typecheck green.

**Design (done):**
- **Lines = stacked logical voices** (`compiler/lines.ts`). `VoiceRun.count` (voices a line stands for), `weight` (loudness), `members` (pool voices merged in). Before a point moment, `expand` lists each line `count` times: the line plus phantoms (dormant pool voices, former members first; synthetic indices ≥ pool size if none). The moment function runs unchanged on these (so unison → 12-note chord still voices 12 notes; examples keep their notes). `collapse` maps back: a line's copies grouped by target (folded in the line's range); the group nearest the line's destination stays on the line, the others **fork** (births from the line, carriers = the line's phantom voices low→high, preferring one whose range holds the pitch), taking count/weight shares; dying copies lower the line's weight (`lose`, ramp over the glide length); whole-line deaths stay deaths. Canonical: independent of which copy got which target.
- **Merge on arrival** (`compile.ts onJoin`, event `SUB_JOIN` at every non-death move end): a line coming to rest on another resting line's pitch merges; the earliest-resting survives (ties: lower pool index), the other fades over `HANDOVER` = 0.1 s following it (M4.5-17 `into`), the survivor takes count, weight (× (level_d/level_s)²) and members, weight ramp over HANDOVER. Covers unison, chord/shift/spread coincidences, pins, note points and overrides onto another line. Start voices on one pitch are one line at t = 0 (`initStart`). Explicit merges/`count` deaths keep the old fade (logical voices die; no weight transfer). Forks fade in over `FORK_FADE` = 0.1 s while the parent's weight ramps down (`onBirth`).
- **Compiled score (additive):** `CompiledVoice.weight?: WeightChange[]` ({t, w, over}, linear ramp; 1 before; absent when unused). `MomentTrace.logical` (logical intents; `intents` are now pool intents); freezeChord reads `logical`.
- **Flat layout v3:** `FlatVoice.weights` (WEIGHT_T/W/OVER, stride 3, `core/segment.ts`). DSP (`voice.ts computeLife`): `lifeTick` = life·√w (amplitude), new `powTick` = life·w; g_n = 1/√Σ powTick (graph.ts). Allocation-free; 1 → unchanged path.
- **Override `weight`** (`overrides[].weight`, schema >0 ≤64, file-format regenerated): sets the weight of the line that voice ends on (ramp over the glide length).
- **Wobble:** `info wobble-ignored` when a piece timbre has wobble > 0. **DSP wobble paths not yet removed** (still disable the lock).
- **Palette/defaults:** `effect.ts readState` expands lines by rounded weight; lines fading out are not active. `query.weightAt`, `weightTargetAt`, `stackedCountAt`; `defaultMoment` counts voices by weight. no-effect reasons count logical voices/deaths.
- Incremental: count, weight, weights length, members checkpointed.

**Goldens changed:** all five examples (`examples.golden.json`), `arrive.test.ts` spread hashes. **Balance:** `tests/golden/balance.test.ts` vs `examples.balance.json` (recorded from the pre-rev-8 compiler; never regenerate): per-pitch power share at resting times within 0.5 dB, all examples pass; times from a walk's start to the next point moment are skipped (walks step lines, not stacked voices, so a doubled voice no longer walks apart: one note differs in clockwork-heron and cartographers there).

**Test changes:** stacked counts via `stackedChordAt` (compiler-moments, octaves), `stackedCountAt` (generate), note property uses `logical`, ranges property allows a chain of interruptions starting in a bézier overshoot, freezeChord sweep skips `free` chords (fork timings differ when frozen ordered), flat transfer list 5 buffers/voice.

**Left:**
1. `tests/property/core/distinct-notes.property.test.ts` "no two resting lines share a pitch" is `it.skip`: fails at seed 2611 (two lines on 42 at 1.25 s). Not diagnosed (suspects: a move ending while the other line still has a pending move that is then cancelled, so no join event fires for either; or walk steps onto a held pitch). A fix may need a join check after a moment cancels pending moves (`reaim` point) and in `walkTick`'s "skip held pitches".
2. Walk steps skipping held pitches: not specifically implemented (strict-between-neighbours already avoids neighbours' destinations; joins catch the rest).
3. DSP: remove/neutralise `wobble` (TimbreParams → 0), unit test that weight w sounds at √w (render one voice w = 4 vs four unlocked lines) and g_n counts weights; MIDI (velocity ∝ √w, or document "not exported"); stems unchanged in principle (per-voice amp carries √w).
4. Renderer thickness by weight (`src/render/draw.ts`, coordinate with M4.5-25), inspector weight field (Overrides rows: number input → `overrides[].weight`).
5. PLAN deviations to fold in: `count` and "Notes: n" count voices (Σ weights), not lines; explicit merge/count deaths do not transfer weight; HANDOVER/FORK_FADE 0.1 s; joins use levels, not voice gains (gains are outside the simulation for incremental compile).
6. Merge origin/main (M4.5-23 `note.changes`, M4.5-26) — not done.

## M4.5-29a: distinct notes, lines counted (core, 2026-11-21)
Merged origin/main (M4.5-23 `note.changes`, M4.5-26 open canvas) into the WIP; `collapse` maps every note change (`plan.notes[]`).

**Changes from the WIP (owner rulings 2026-11-21):**
- **Counts count lines.** `split`, `merge` and a chord with `count` see each line once (`lines.ts countsLines`, `expand(…, flat)`); moments without a count still see a line `count` times. A newborn whose parent stands for several voices forks off with an even share of them (parent first, newborns low to high); past the parent's count, newborns are fresh (weight 1). `defaultMoment`, palette reasons and no-effect reasons of count moments read lines; the other moments' reasons read the voices they saw. `stackedCountAt` → `linesAt` (lines heard) + `stackAt`. Inspector "Notes: n" already read `activeAt` (lines): unchanged.
- **Every join passes weight.** Merge and `count` deaths hand their weight to their survivor when their fade starts (`SUB_HANDOVER` event, `handOver`; a zero fade hands over at the close; a survivor already handing on passes it along). Coincidence joins also pass the stack count; explicit merges do not (a later chord without count still sees the lines that remain).
- **More lines than notes.** A newborn or fork bound for a note its own parent or a sibling from the same parent takes is not born: its voices and weight join that line (`Collapsed.absorb`; counted as births for no-effect). Bound for another line's note, it glides there and merges on arrival.
- **Chords take free octaves before merging lines** (owner, later ruling): `chord.ts distinctTargets`, for `chord`/`degrees` (not `pitches`/`current`, not hand edits), any voicing, with or without count: missing targets take free octaves (`fillOctaves`) in the lines' own range (an explicit register may be narrower), then targets on a note an untargeted line holds or another target takes (Nearest) move to the nearest free chord tone in range; targets re-sorted so lines keep order. Merge only when nothing is free. Dying lines of a count chord don't count as holding.
- **Compiled score (additive):** `CompiledVoice.stack?: StackChange[]` (voices a line stands for), read by the palette's stacked view (`StateAt.stacked`) instead of rounded weights. Suggestions use the stacked view.

**Left #1 (seed 2611) fixed.** Causes: (a) a line arriving where another rests while both already have pending moves to one destination: `onJoin` now joins when the committed destinations match, dropping the dying line's pending moves; (b) no join check after a moment cancels moves or at a birth: queued at the moment (targeted lines left holding) and at each birth; (c) a newborn whose move a later moment replaced waited on its parent's pitch: its birth moves to its new departure; (d) a flat 0-motion segment counted as busy; (e) fork weight subtracted twice when the parent was born after the fork was decided (`Birth.forkAt`; births take the committed weight). Property: lines may share a pitch only while one leaves within 10 s (a later moment's staggered moves already part them); dying lines found from the trace. 4×1000 runs green.
**Left #2:** walk steps already land strictly between neighbours' committed destinations, so never on a held note; test added (`lines-counted.test.ts`).

**Decisions:** surplus notes add weight to the line on that note (above); transitional shared rests allowed as above; HANDOVER/FORK_FADE 0.1 s and level-scaled joins kept.
**Balance test:** explicit merges now pass weight, so times from a `merge`/`count` chord to the next unison are skipped too (cartographers 81.5–105.5 s); the reference is untouched.
**Goldens:** all five examples (`examples.golden.json`, `arrive.test.ts`); diagnostics unchanged.
**Tests:** `lines-counted.test.ts`, `chord-octaves.test.ts` (narrow register, no free pitch, Nearest, short list, count, literal list), distinct-notes property un-skipped + "chords take free octaves"; updated no-effect, shortcuts, generate (Σ stack), chord-count-lines (stacked line forks), stacked fixtures (`stackAt`).
**Incremental fix:** checkpoints keep the last weight/stack entry (`rampWeight`/`setCount` replace it in the shared arrays after a checkpoint; incremental ≠ full at 1000 runs, path 574).
**Seeds at 1000 runs** (compile-time budget lifted only for these checks, load ~30): ranges 811138243 and walk-over-point 112518007 now pass; crossings 512133634/31 and chord-count far side 439040815/857 still fail; crossings −1185324365 now fails earlier (path 156: a shift after a pin fork, v7/v9) — not diagnosed; new: `note moments` 2611/332 (an `order` note's intents out of order after it cancels pending moves of two lines; looks like the pre-cancel/post-cancel sort in `applyPointMoment`, not diagnosed). Only compile-time budget failures otherwise (machine loaded).
**For 29b:** DSP wobble removal and √w unit test, MIDI, inspector weight field, line thickness (read `weight`; `stack` is available for "×n" marks if wanted).

## M4.5-30: spread at range edges (core, 2026-11-21)
- **Cause confirmed:** `c + factor·(p − c)` sends outer lines past the range; the compiler folds them by octave (§7.8) into the cluster. Owner-like case (D dorian, [36, 84], 38 41 45 72 76 79, mean, ×1.8) gave 45 38 47 83 77 83 (`range-fold`): crossings and a doubled 83. Now 36 40 43 76 81 84.
- **Rule** (`moments/spread.ts`, factor ≥ 0; negative factors unchanged): voices sharing a destination are one note and stay together. Each side of c gets one scale k ≤ 1 (the largest that keeps every voice on that side in its range), so the binding voice lands on its edge and the rest scale with it; then clamp (no fold), snap inside the range, and if any targets coincide, nudge to the nearest free steps (isotonic fit in scale/semitone steps, ties down) with a forward/backward pass against the bounds. `snap: 'none'` keeps exact targets unless two coincide (then the semitone grid). If the ranges can't hold the notes apart: new `spread-crowded` (warn, registered in `compiler/diagnostics.ts`) and some coincide (the compiler merges them).
- **Tests:** unit `core/spread.test.ts` (owner case through the compiler: no crossings, in range, order, wider, distinct scale tones, edges reached; one-side compression; squeeze nudge; crowded range; unisons stay one note; mirror); property `property/core/spread.property.test.ts` (`PROP_SEED`/`PROP_RUNS`; per-voice ranges, uniform ranges with capacity ⇒ ok, widening, compiler-level order/range/distinct/no `range-fold`). 3000 random runs pass.
- **Goldens:** only Lanterns over Salt Flats changed: `LnSpr1` (×1.6 around the median 63.5, range 38–84) folded its lowest and top lines and crossed; it now compresses both sides to the edges. Regenerated `examples.golden.json`; `arrive.test.ts`'s pre-rev-6 hash for Lanterns updated (noted inline).

## M4.5-32: arrival artifact (dsp, 2026-11-21)
- **Cause: the articulation strike.** Per-block traces of every voice at every arrival of the five examples (all glass): the only change at an arrival is articulation. Amplitude rises +53 % (0.65 → 1.0, +3.7 dB) in 4 ms, a linear attack evaluated at control rate, with corners at its start and peak (second difference 0.35 of the level per block; 1e-4 with articulation off). Nothing else moves: pitch and phase increment are smooth (≤ 0.004 cents of second difference), λ = 0, h = 1, the limiter never engages (peaks −8 to −16 dBFS). With `arrive: 'together'` every moving voice strikes on the same sample, so the ticks add up.
- **Numbers** (`tests/dsp/arrival.test.ts`: four sines with glass's articulation, chord to chord, 3 kHz high-pass ÷ full band in 2 ms windows, worst window in [−5, +20] ms of an arrival ÷ the steady windows): main 4.3–7.8× (together), 3.1–9.4× (spread). A smootherstep attack alone, still 4 ms: 2.4–8.7×, because the 16-frame control-rate interpolation puts corners in a fast curve too. Smootherstep with an attack of 8 / 10 / 12 / 15 ms: worst 3.6 / 2.4 / 1.3 / 1.7×. Fixed: 0.46–0.93× (together), 0.55–1.29× (spread).
- **Fix** (`voice.ts`, `wavetable.ts`, `constants.ts`): the attack is a smootherstep from the still-decaying envelope (the "linear attack" above is replaced), and it lasts at least `MIN_ARTICULATION_ATTACK` = 12 ms, as `MIN_LIFE_FADE` floors life fades. `process()` stays allocation-free.
- **Deviation, for the owner's ear:** the floor changes three presets. Glass 4 → 12 ms and organ 3 → 12 ms; pluck 2 → 12 ms, so it plucks more softly. A pluck strikes 90 % and still measures 9–25× at 12 ms; it would need about 60 ms to be under 2×, so the test covers glass's 35 % only. Reed, choir and wobble are unchanged (20 ms and more). The timbre files keep their values. If the owner wants a crisp pluck or key click back, the floor could apply only to strikes of up to ~50 %, or the presets could change in core.
- Not the cause: glide shapes (all four eases end cleanly), block rounding of segment ends, phase resets, the mip or slot crossfade, the unison lock or wobble (no exact unisons at the examples' arrivals), the motion accent and the vibrato onset (smooth), and the worklet, which only hosts the graph.

## M4.5-33: Shift-drag by octaves (app, 2026-11-21)
- **Modifiers:** Shift = whole octaves from the start pitch; Alt = free on both axes (chromatic, off the grid). Applies to dot drags (`ArrivalDots.tsx`) and note-point drags (`canvas-input.ts`). Pressing or releasing Shift/Alt mid-drag re-applies the last pointer position (key listeners for the drag's life). Shift on press still adds an unselected dot to the group; a Shift-click without movement still toggles it.
- **Math** (`editor/octave-drag.ts`, pure): `octaveSpan` is the octave interval every dragged dot can move and stay in its range (0 always included, so a dot already out of range never blocks the rest); `octaveSnap` rounds to the nearest octave and clamps to it. `DotsDrag.octaves(raw)` returns k, the grabbed pitch and the guides; the move then goes through `to(…, chromatic = true)`, so every dot moves by exactly 12k.
- **Keys:** Shift+▲/▼ moves the selected dots an octave (`octaveDots`, one undo entry, they stay selected), else the selected note point (`stepNote(…, octave)`). Plain ▲/▼ unchanged.
- **Feedback:** `octave-guide.ts` state, drawn as DOM in the dots overlay (no `src/render` change): dashed accent lines at each reachable octave of the start, and a "+1 oct" / "−2 oct" label by the dot; `--accent`/`--paper` tokens, so both themes. Menu help updated.

## Compositions: three songs in the examples menu (owner, 2026-11-20)
- **What:** the owner asked for demos that sound like music. Three songs, written with the instrument (melody by pins, chords, bass line, phrasing levels, pulses as repeating level patterns): Harbour Song, Night Ferry, Kite Day, in `examples/compositions/` (CC0). The menu shows two groups, **Originals** (the five first examples) and **Compositions** (owner's names). `examples.ts` exports `ORIGINALS`, `COMPOSITIONS`, and `EXAMPLES` (both, for the featured lookup).
- **Deviation:** PLAN §11.1 says 4–6 examples; there are now 8, in two groups. The originals' tests (golden, arrive, chord-count, incremental, suggestions, MIDI) still iterate the top-level folder only; `tests/unit/core/compositions.test.ts` covers the songs (parse, compile with no warn/error, no line dies before the ending).
- **Trap for songwriting under rev 8 (distinct notes):** a melody pin landing on a pitch another line holds merges the two and the melody line dies, losing the rest of the tune. The songs keep the melody's register clear of chords and the walk; the test above guards it.
- **Other gaps met while writing them** (for the owner/integrator): a song needs ~300–1,100 level moments to fake pulses or an arpeggio; level spikes don't re-strike a timbre's articulation, so a held pluck voice stays near silent; split and merge ignore stagger; silent (level 0) voices still count in the 1/√n normalisation.
- Generator scripts (Python, not part of the app): owner's `renders/demos-2026-11-20/songs/generators/`.
- Gates: lint, typecheck, build, `pnpm test` (export/stems/incremental timeouts at load 35 passed alone), e2e examples gallery + menu on Chromium and Firefox.

## M4.5-29b: weights in sound, MIDI and view (2026-11-21)
- **DSP.** √w amplitude and g_n = 1/√Σ(life·w) were already in the 29 WIP (`voice.ts computeLife`); now tested (`tests/dsp/weight.test.ts`: stem amplitude ÷ weight-1 = √w within 1 % for w = 0.5…8; a weight-3 line leaves another line's g_n as three separate lines do; ramps followed).
- **Wobble removed from the engine.** `TimbreParams.wobble`, the slot field and every wobble path in `voice.ts`/`graph.ts` are gone; `detuneCents(spread)` lost its wobble argument. Lockability stays as plumbing (`VoiceContext.lockable`, always 1): the lock still serves lines that meet or cross mid-glide and the 0.1 s hand-over of a merge. `Graph.setLockable` / `RenderInput.unlocked` turn it off for the tests' no-lock reference, which used to be a `wobble: 2.75` twin timbre. Removed: the "wobble voices keep beating" test, the wobble detune test and the `wobble-8`/`wobble-phrase` listening fixtures; `preset-wobble` stays (the preset keeps its name and sound, minus the beating). Core comments only in `types.ts`/`timbres.ts`; the schema description is untouched (file-format unchanged).
- **MIDI** (`midi/export.ts weightVelocity`): note-on velocity = base·√w at the weight the note settles on (target of the ramp in effect, half a tick of slack). If base·√(peak weight) > 127, every velocity scales by one factor so ratios hold (weight 8 at base 100: 127 vs 45 for weight 1); pieces without weights export as before. Mid-note weight changes are not written (no CC for them). `tests/unit/midi/velocity.test.ts`.
- **Render** (`draw.ts weightWidth`): width = base·√w, w clamped to [0.25, 16] (1.5 px at 1, 3 px at 4, 4.2 px at 8); highlights use the same curve from 2.75 px. A voice with a weight timeline strokes one path per constant-weight run and a ramp in ~6 px steps (≤ 16); voices without one take the old single-path path. No "×n" mark: a stack count beside the Hz label read as several notes, against "the artist thinks in notes". `tests/unit/render/weight.test.ts`.
- **Inspector.** Storage: the WIP's `overrides[].weight` (already in types/schema/compiler), kept: it is per moment and per line like the other hand edits, and needs no new field. Each chord note row has a weight box (`WeightBox`: type + Enter, ↑/↓ ±0.25, Shift ±1, drag "×" to scrub; one undo entry per commit, press or drag; editing a weight does not freeze the chord). The row's line is the one resting on that note just after the chord's span end (`editor/overrides.ts noteLines`); the box shows the note's weight, joins on arrival included, and writes override = asked − joined (`weightForNote`, ≥ 0.25). The Overrides list shows "weight n".
- **Core (small):** `compiler/overrides.ts`: a weight-only override no longer makes the plan unordered or stops a dying voice following its survivor (only pitch/end edits do), so a weight changes no path. `app/editor/overrides.ts withOverride` keeps `weight` when a dot drag edits pitch/end (it dropped it).
- **Goldens:** unchanged (no example has a piece timbre with `wobble`; `wobble-ignored` already came with 29a).
- **Tests:** above, plus `tests/unit/core/weight-override.test.ts`, `tests/unit/app/note-weight.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/note-weight.spec.ts` (chromium-dev and -prod green).

## M4.5-35: drag preview and auditioning (app, 2026-11-21)
- **Silent previews** (`preview.ts`): palette and suggestion hovers no longer touch the engine. A preview compiles the piece with the probe moment and the loop draws that score's lines from the moment's time, dashed over the real ones (`ScoreStyle.preview`, new layer in `render/draw.ts`; `frameStats.preview` is its start time, NaN when none). Shown during playback too; any piece change clears it. There is no score context menu yet, so nothing to change there.
- **Drag preview** (`palette-drop.ts`): the palette's dragstart records the type (dragover can't read the data); dragover on the score or lane previews at the snapped drop time, recompiling only when that time changes. Leaving the target (not onto a child), dragend and Escape clear it; Escape also marks the drag cancelled, so a following drop is ignored (Playwright's drag doesn't cancel natively).
- **Engine** (`AudioEngine.audition/endAudition`): an audition swaps a tiny score into the worklet and plays it from 0, on the normal voice path; no worklet or `src/dsp` change. While it holds the engine, `swap` keeps the real score (tables still sent) and the loop range, `ended` is ignored, and `play`/`pause`/`seek` end it first. `endAudition` pauses and swaps the last real score back. Refused while the transport plays.
- **Audition score** (`audition.ts`): every pool voice (ids match for the swap), the auditioned ones holding their pitch with the timbre, level, weight and stack they have at that time, gain/pan/mute/solo/sends copied; 1.5 s one-shot with a 0.3 s fade, or held (30 s cap) for drags. Each new audition replaces the last: a queued one that's no longer the latest is skipped, and a stopped one is ended as soon as it starts.
- **Hooks:** chord picks (diatonic, root, quality, bass), voicing and degrees in the inspector play the moment's resulting notes once it has recompiled (`auditionAfterEdit`, 500 ms fallback); `stepNote` (▲/▼ on note points, canvas and inspector) plays the stepped notes; dot drags and note-point drags hold the snapped pitch (Shift octaves, Alt chromatic, as the drag computes it) and stop on release; a plain click on a moment in the lane (pointer or Enter) or on the score plays what it moves to (`momentNotes`: destinations at the span end, thinned voices left out). Not hooked: walk-step and chord-note ▲/▼ in the inspector (other builders' area), Shift+▲/▼ on dots.
- Play, scrubbing and loads stop an audition; a seek while stopped doesn't.
- **Tests:** `tests/unit/app/audition.test.ts`; `tests/e2e/audition.spec.ts`. `context-moments.spec` and `suggestions.spec` now check that hover is silent (ghost shown, engine not playing).
- **Gates** (load average 250–360): lint, typecheck green; `tests/unit/app` 272/272. Full `pnpm test`: 1161/1183, the 22 failures are dsp/export tests over their own timeouts, compile-time asserts and compiler property counterexamples, all outside this change (no `src/core` or `src/dsp` edit). `test:perf`: no-gc passes; RTF (8.7×) and compile median fail on load; the engine change is main-thread only. e2e on chromium-dev and -prod: the new spec and the affected ones (context-moments, suggestions, palette, editor-polish, dots, octave-drag, inspector, lane, score-select, app) pass; app.spec's first-sound and play tests timed out once on dev under load and pass alone.
## M4.5 property seeds after 29 (core, 2026-11-21)
Seeds from 29a and its merge (budget lifted only locally, never committed). Compiler fixes in `compiler/compile.ts`, `plan.ts`, `project.ts`; regressions in `tests/unit/core/property-seeds-29.test.ts` and two new fixtures in `tests/property/core/regressions/`. Goldens and diagnostics of all five examples unchanged.

| Seed | Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| note 2611/332, −1185324365/151 | An `order` note is planned in the pre-cancel order (where lines were heading), but `applyPointMoment` then re-sorted `x.active` by the post-cancel destinations, and `collapse` lists pool intents in that order: a line whose pending move was cancelled (and re-planned to the same target) came out of place. The plan itself was right (the planner sorts by target). | **Compiler:** the note keeps `x.active` in its pre-cancel order. |
| note 512133634/732 | A stacked line's copy sent below the range is folded per logical voice in the trace; that copy could not fork (pool exhausted), so no pool target folded and no `range-fold` was raised. The property used the diagnostic as its only fold signal. | **Trace (additive):** `MomentTrace.logical[].folded`. **Property** (wrong under rev 8: logical voices fold without a pool fold) also skips folded logical targets. |
| crossings 512133634/31 | M4.5-17 as designed: a count death's glide starts after a later unordered spread moved its survivor, so it aims at the survivor's new note and passes a line on the way. | **Property:** a later moment that takes over a dying voice's survivor ends the window for the dying voice too. Not rev 8; exposed now. |
| crossings −1185324365/156 (and 512133634/479 found on the way) | A pin forks a copy off a stacked line; a shift cancels the fork's move before its birth (§7.1), so fork and parent both go to 72 and join there. They touch out of order by ~1e-6 st as they meet. | **Property** (wrong under rev 8): adjacent lines sent to one note end as one line, like two deaths into one survivor; exempt. |
| chord-count 439040815/857 (far side) | Forks born together on a parent mid walk step, with a steep-start bézier: m₀ = v·d − Δ·e′(0) differs by each newborn's interval, so the higher-bound one dipped below its neighbour right after birth. The planner saw "out of order at the first common sample" and left it as not its doing. | **Compiler** (`plan.ts`): such a pair (newborn of this moment born since the last sample, same timing group) is tightened (m₀ scaled down) before it is given up. |
| chord-count 439040815/984 | v6 joins v1 (rev 8) and closes while v7/v8 still die into v6; a later chord reuses v6 as a newborn and v7/v8 followed it through the other lines (and would have handed it their weight). | **Compiler** (`onClose` → `passOn`): when a line that merged into another closes, lines dying into it (and their pending death glides) point at its survivor. |
| chord-count 439040815/429 | A spread forks a copy of v6 (hand-set pitch) while v6 glides onto v4's note; v6 joins and closes before the fork's staggered birth, so the fork was born in mid-air. | **Compiler** (`passOn`): forks still waiting on it fork from its survivor, on the note it joined. |
| chord-count 1987819535/617 (new, found by the sweep; fails on main too) | The unison forks a copy of a stacked newborn (v4) before v4 is born (its birth moved to its new departure, 29a (c)); the fork found no sounding parent and was born in mid-air on its target. | **Compiler:** a newborn whose parent is not born yet starts from that parent's parent (where the parent still is): `onBirth`, and the planner's projection (`Projector.birthState`, also `birthPitch`). |
| chord-count 512133634/367 (new, sweep) | Two chords at one time: the second forks a line off a fresh newborn of the first, drawn to start before that parent sounds; born in mid-air. | **Compiler** (`plan.ts`): a newborn whose parent is not born yet is deferred to the parent's birth (`deferT`). |
| crossings 439040815/675 (new, sweep; fails on main too) | A newborn of an earlier moment re-planned before its birth is born when its new move departs (29a (c)), but the planner projected it born at the old time on its parent's pitch, so it missed the newborn passing its neighbour after the real birth. | **Compiler** (`project.ts`): the projection moves such a birth to the first new move's departure, as the compiler does. |
| chord-count −41577023/608 (new, sweep) | A merge kills v1 into v4, its own fork not born yet; v1 closed first, so v4 appeared on its target with nothing sounding there and v1's weight went nowhere. | **Compiler** (`handOver`): a survivor not born yet is born when the weight passes, on the dying line's note. |
| walk over point 512133634/499 | The property took the moves of a note's return (a second trace entry, same id) for the first run's pending move, which a later note had cancelled. | **Property:** a run's own move must start before the moment's next run. |

**Cartographers `crossing-untargeted` at `CrChd2`: not the example's content; the 29a rule.** `CrChd2` (bVII close, register [60, 84], upper lines) reaches 8 voices (one line stacked); the register has 6 free bVII tones (62 67 71 74 79 83). `distinctTargets` then takes the nearest free chord tones in the lines' own range [38, 84], ignoring the register and the untargeted lines: 59 and 43, below the 8 untargeted lines, which those two moves cross. Before 29a the extra targets doubled in the register and merged. Not changed (an owner ruling, "in range"). **Open, for the integrator:** for a subset target, take free octaves only within the register (or between the untargeted neighbours) and merge otherwise; that would also remove the new diagnostic.

**Not changed:** incremental = full (property at 1000 runs below); `CompiledVoice`/flat untouched.

**Status / handoff (builder stopped near 200k context, load ~100–300):**
- Every listed seed and the three sweep finds pass at 1000 runs (compiler + chord-count properties, budget lifted locally): 512133634, −1185324365, 2611, 439040815, 1987819535. Unit regressions red without each compiler fix (checked by toggling).
- Goldens, `tests/unit/core`, lint, typecheck green. `pnpm test`: only DSP/audio tests time out under load (`tests/dsp/{graph,stems,unison}`, `tests/unit/audio/stems`, all `TestTimeoutError`, none touched here).
- **Left:** (1) seed −41577023 path 907 fails "overlap: walk step cuts m00000 at 22.9" (v5): not diagnosed; may predate this branch, may come from the hand-over birth fix (check with that block disabled). (2) The 4×1000 random sweep did not complete (each run found a new seed; the last fixes are not swept). (3) `pnpm test:perf` not run (machine loaded); `passOn`/`handOver` add a per-close loop over voices, the projector a `find` per pending newborn. (4) Cartographers `CrChd2` ruling above.
## M4.5-36: key changes carry forward (2026-11-21)
- **Core** `core/transpose.ts`: `remapPitch(p, from, to)` keeps the degree at or below p and its octave (counted from the tonic), adds the chromatic offset, moves the tonic the shorter way (tritone down). Scales of different sizes map each old degree to the new scale's nearest interval (or the octave), ties down. Integer steps are added to p, so fractions survive. `remapSymbol`: letter root and note bass transposed and spelled in the new key when only the tonic changes; null (→ pitch list) when the scale changes; Roman heads stay, a note bass after one is remapped. `remapMoment` covers pitch lists, named chords (scale change: the pitches it voices, via a `voiced` callback; none → `skipped`), frozen chords (`frozenFrom` too; a named one the scale change can't rename gets the frozen pitches), frozen walks, note points (`from`, `to`, `changes`), pins and numeric unisons, `overrides[].pitch`. Pitches tied to one voice (pins, walk notes, overrides) fold into that voice's range and are reported (`folds`). `fixedNoteMoments(piece, keyId)`: moments after the key in compile order (`at`, then array order) up to the next unmuted key; muted moments count. `keyBefore`, `carryKey`.
- **App**: `history.amend(entry, recipe)` joins the newest undo entry when it is still `entry` (from `lastEntry()`), else records its own. `editor/key-carry.ts` holds the prompt: offered by `addMoment('key')` (old key = `keyBefore`) and by the inspector's tonic/scale edits (old key = the moment's previous key). Closes on Move/Keep or a selection change; a piece change that alters or removes the key (undo, another edit) closes it but remembers its old key, so editing again asks from the key the notes are still in. Move uses `freezeChord` for named chords under a scale change and posts an info notice for folds or skipped chords. Banner: `inspector/KeyCarryBanner.tsx` + `key-carry.css`, rendered under `KeyFields` only. "until the end" when no key follows.
- **Suggestions**: Home writes `unison` `pitch: 'tonic'` (checked to resolve to the same note as before). **Not key-relative, needs schema v2 (M4.5-27):** Collapse to a non-tonic chord tone stays a numeric unison: a one-degree chord in an octave window was tried, but degree chords now take free octaves before unisons (owner 2026-11-21), so it spreads instead of collapsing. Thin stays a pitch list: no NoteSet says "these three notes in these octaves" for five or more voices. Both get the prompt.
- Note points' `from` is remapped too; if the note it matches was sounding before the key and is held, the remapped `from` no longer matches (`note-not-sounding`). Accepted per the task.
- **Tests**: `unit/core/transpose.test.ts`, `unit/app/key-carry.test.ts` (amend, prompt flow, undo), `unit/core/suggest.test.ts` (Home is 'tonic' and sounds like the numeric unison; follows a key change); e2e `key-carry.spec.ts` (Clockwork Heron: add E lydian at 70 s, Move, Amaj7#11 / pitches −5 / degree chord untouched, one undo restores; Keep).

## M4.5-37: Space always plays and pauses (app, 2026-11-21)
- **Decision** (`src/app/space-key.ts`, pure): `spaceKey(event, held, {editor, dialog})` returns `{consume, toggle}`. Text fields (`typesSpace`: text-like inputs incl. search and number, textarea, contenteditable; not select) pass. Otherwise keydown and keyup are both consumed. Player layout toggles on keydown; the editor arms on keydown and toggles on release unless a Space-drag panned (rev 7, unchanged: `canvas-input.ts` still sets `space.panned`). Repeats never toggle. A held Space toggles on release even if focus moved into a text field.
- **Wiring** (`canvas-input.ts` `attachKeys`): one window capture-phase listener for keydown and keyup; on consume it calls `preventDefault` and `stopPropagation`, so no button, chip, checkbox, select, lane item or React handler sees Space. The old document-level Space branch and its button exception are gone.
- **Open dialog:** Space is left to the browser (dialog buttons and fields work as before), as the old handler did. A hold armed before the dialog opened ends without toggling.
- **Enter replaces Space:** buttons and chips (degree picker, chord chips, mute chips, palette) activate with Enter natively. Checkboxes and radios have no native Enter, so the same window handler makes Enter click a focused checkbox or radio. Lane items select with Enter only (`Lane.tsx`).
- **Not done:** musical typing (§9) isn't built yet; when it is, Space stays play/pause through this handler.
- **Tests:** `tests/unit/app/space-key.test.ts` (targets, typing, repeat, Space-drag, dialog); `canvas-input.test.ts` Space cases updated; new `tests/e2e/space-key.spec.ts` (transport, palette, slider, degree chip, Muted checkbox + Enter, Notes drawer toggle, mute chip and timbre select, app menu; Label field types a space; lane Enter selects, Space doesn't); `inspector.spec.ts` degree picker now toggles with Enter.
- **Status (load 90–300):** lint, typecheck green. `pnpm test --maxWorkers=2`: 1179/1183; the 4 failures are `tests/unit/audio/stems.test.ts` timeouts (own per-test limits), not touched here. E2E (`--workers=1`): space-key, inspector degree picker, score-select Space-drag and app Space green on chromium-dev and chromium-prod; space-key and app Space green on firefox-dev. In the parallel run Firefox failed at `page.goto` timeouts across specs (load).
## R9-5: silent lines vanish (render, 2026-11-21)
- **Opacity** (`src/render/level.ts`): `opacityAt(v, t)` = level timeline × life fade (smootherstep, as the DSP) × `pulseLevel`, clamped 0..1; `VoiceDef.gain` is not in it. **Pulse hook:** `pulseLevel` returns 1; R9-3a evaluates its hit records there and adds the hit times to `opacityBreaks`.
- **Precompute:** `opacityBreaks(v)` (births, deaths, fade ends, level ramp ends) is cached per compiled voice (WeakMap, rechecked against the `level`/`lives` arrays). `null` when the line is at full level wherever it sounds (no level changes, fades ≤ 5 ms): the old single-path stroke.
- **Drawing** (`draw.ts traceWeighted`): cuts at weight and opacity breaks; a constant run is one stroke at alpha × opacity, a ramp is ~6 px steps (≤ 16), butt-capped in an opacity ramp so the seams don't bead; runs under 1 % are skipped. Alpha multiplies the existing dim (`DIM_ALPHA`, ghost, highlight); widths untouched. Heads, rings and Hz labels skip silent voices and fade with the line.
- **Dotted trace** (editor only, `ScoreStyle.traces`): where a selected or hovered line is below 25 % opacity, a 1.25 px dotted line at 0.55. Level changes carry no moment id, so a selected `level` moment matches a voice's level change by its span (start = t, end = t + over), traced from there; segment-owning moments trace their highlighted runs. `loop.ts` passes the hovered voice (`traceVoice`, redraw on change) and exposes `frameStats.view` for pixel e2e.
- **Hover/selection:** `nearestLine` is geometric, so silent lines stay hoverable and clickable in the editor; new `audibleOnly` argument (the player passes it, so no readout over empty paper).
- **Tests:** `tests/unit/render/level.test.ts`; `tests/e2e/silent-lines.spec.ts` (level 0 removes the pixels, a ramp draws rising contrast, selection and hover show the trace).

## E2E: note-points flake and NaN input (2026-11-21)
- **note-points flake (app bug):** the first click of a double-click on a line no moment owns seeks, and the camera follows the seek. When a frame ran between the presses, the second press read the same screen x in the moved view (10.2 → 13.4 s; camera start 0 → 3.2). Load made that frame likely. Fix (`canvas-input.ts`): a score click's seek holds the camera for a frame, as the ruler's does, so a click never scrolls the view and the double-click stays where it started. The spec now waits two frames between the presses and checks that the view didn't move, so without the fix it fails every time.
- **NaN `value` warning (spec data):** `lane-rows.spec.ts` loaded a shift moment with `steps: 1` and no `by`, which the schema requires (`loadPiece` skips validation). Once the test selected it, M4.5-31's `ShiftSlider` got `value={NaN}`. The moment now has `by: 1, unit: 'scaleSteps'`, and the spec fails on any console error.

## M4.5 polish: held notes in key carry, more auditions (2026-11-21)
- **Held notes** (`core/transpose.ts carryKey`): a note point's `to`s are always remapped; each `from` only when its line gets that pitch in the span. Decided on compiled scores: `before` = the piece with the key moment set back to the old key (what the note was written against) picks the matched voice, as the compiler matches (nearest destination or pitch at t within ½ semitone, one voice per change; targeting not checked); `after` = the carried piece so far (incremental compiler, notes in compile order) says whether that voice now sounds the old pitch (held from before the key: keep `from`) or the remapped one (remap). So a fixed chord in the span that restates a held note moves it and its `from` follows; a note after an earlier in-span note follows that one. A `from` that matches nothing is remapped, as before. Replaces M4.5-36's accepted `note-not-sounding`.
- **Auditions** (`audition.ts`, hooks in `editor/actions.ts`): `afterEdit` is the shared wait-for-compile (newer call replaces, refused while playing). `moveWalkStep` → `auditionStepAfterEdit` (the step's voice at its compiled `to`, at the step's time); `moveChordNote` → `auditionAfterEdit(id, [pitch])`; `octaveDots` → `auditionPointsAfterEdit(landed)` (each dot's voice, else the nearest sounding one; at the grabbed dot's time). Ghost previews and silent lines unchanged.
- **Tests:** `unit/core/transpose.test.ts` (held vs in-span `from`, grouped changes, a later note, a restating chord; no `note-not-sounding` after); `unit/app/audition.test.ts` (walk step, chord note with replacement, Shift+▲ dots, silent while playing).
- **Status:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=2` 1263/1263. E2E chromium-dev and -prod (`--workers=1`): key-carry, audition, inspector, octave-drag 48/48.

## M4.5 property seeds 2 + chord register (core, 2026-11-21)
Regressions in `tests/unit/core/property-seeds-29.test.ts` (the three for compiler fixes red on main, checked); compile budget lifted only locally.

| Seed | Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| overlap −41577023/907 | Not the hand-over birth fix: fails identically before the 29 seeds work. A merge at 22.9 kills v5 (mid pin glide) into v3 while the walk steps v3 at 22.9. `follow` replaces v5's death glide as it starts, so no `m00004` segment is left and v5's next segment is the walk's; the property's `isFollow` needed a death glide before the follow. | **Property** (wrong under M4.5-17): a follow starting with the death glide counts too. Compiler unchanged; the test pins the behaviour. |
| chord-count 599296392/298 | v9 dies into v10 and glides to 64, v10's pending (staggered) move; two later moments replace that move with later ones. `reaim` skipped survivors with pending moves, so v9 faded on 64 at its old time while v10 still sat on 95 (stranded in mid-air). | **Compiler** (`reaim`): a dying voice off its survivor's pitch whose survivor has new pending moves fades only once the survivor arrives (`arrivalOf`); `follow` takes it along when they start. Applying this also when the dying line already sits on the survivor kept Cartographers' `CrMrg1` lines alive 18 s; hence the pitch condition. |
| incremental −1808251847/498, 128577514/91 | A checkpoint between a walk's moment event and its first tick (both at `at`; tick 0 is queued by the setup, so `snapshot` skips it) saw no live event of the walk, so `restore` took the old run's final span, which only grows: the new span end was too late. | **Compiler** (`incremental.ts snapshot`): a queued tick 0 of a handled walk keeps its span live. |
| spread −625473937/387 | Crowded spread (`ok: false`) clamped each voice to its own range, which with per-voice ranges put v4 (range from 43) on 45 above v5 on 42. | **Compiler** (`spread.ts inRangeInOrder`): clamp to the bounds every ordered choice has, then a running maximum; per-voice clamp only when no ordered choice exists. |

`tests/property/core`: `PROP_PATH` replay for compiler, incremental and spread; incremental writes the edit history to `$DUMP_DIR/incremental.json` on a mismatch.

**Sweeps:** after the fixes, 4 × `PROP_SEED=random PROP_RUNS=1000` over `tests/property/core` green (40 tests each; incremental 1000 sequences, ~5300 edits). Incremental = full holds.

**Chord register rule** (integrator, `owner-decisions.md` "Free octaves stay inside the chord's register"; `chord.ts octaveRoom`, `distinctTargets`). Applies when the chord has an explicit `register` and untargeted lines sound; otherwise both rooms are the lines' range (29a, unchanged). Free octaves (`fillOctaves`) go first inside the register (∩ lines' range); then into the room beyond it, bounded below by the highest untargeted line under the register *or* under the lowest targeted line (committed destinations), and above likewise; then merge. Collision moves and still-missing targets take a free chord tone inside the register first, then in that room. **Reading of "between the untargeted neighbours":** bounding by lines below the register only was not enough for `CrChd2`: v4 and v6 (untargeted) hold 60 and 61 inside the register, under the targeted lines, and 59 still crossed them.
- **Cartographers `CrChd2`:** 7 lines (8 voices), 6 free bVII notes in [60, 84], no room below (lines at 60, 61) or above (range ends at 84): v13 and v14 both take 79 and merge; the stacked line stays stacked. `crossing-untargeted` is gone.
- **Goldens** (`UPDATE_GOLDEN=1`; `arrive.test.ts` hash from the received value): only Cartographers changes. `CrChd2` targets 62 67 71 74 79 79 83 instead of 43 59 62 67 71 74 83 plus a fork to 79; v14 then dies there, so later moments use other pool voices for the same pitches (`4sXeYw` unison, `AfK2Fm` 48/60/69, `NeAQNT` 47/59/67, `UAz5ZL` 57); duration 176.35 → 176.48 s; diagnostics now empty. The `reaim`, incremental and spread fixes change no golden.
- **Tests:** `chord-octaves.test.ts` "free octaves stay inside the chord's register": CrChd2-like full register (merge), room between register and neighbour (55, not 52), no room (merge), untargeted line inside the register bounds the room, with count. All five fail on main.

**Gates:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=2` 1265/1265 (load ~6). `pnpm test:perf` green: compile median 24.9 ms (min 20.6, max 30.7; budget 100), RTF 17.2× CPU / 17.0× wall, no-GC 0 / 0, expo/bézier 18 scavenges per 60 s (known, M6). `CompiledVoice`/flat untouched.

## Fix: number field keeps a stale draft over undo (2026-11-21)
A focused NumberField (and WeightBox) kept its typed text through an outside undo, and its blur then re-committed it over the undone value.
- `draftReduce` (`inspector/fields.ts`): typing makes a draft; a changed underlying value drops it and shows the new value; blur commits only a draft. NumberField and WeightBox use it via `useReducer`. The Voices/Notes drawer `NumberField` clears its draft when `value` changes.
- Tests: `tests/unit/app/number-draft.test.ts`; `tests/e2e/number-field-undo.spec.ts` (type, undo via the editor hook, blur: undone value stays; an undisturbed draft still commits). The app has no Undo button, and a real click would blur first, so the hook stands in.
- The inspector.spec workaround (blur after Enter) is no longer needed but left alone. TextField (chord symbol) has the same pattern; not changed (not a number field).

## Edit latency: click → swap (app, 2026-11-21)
CI (2 vCPU) missed the 50 ms gate on inspector clicks (`clickChord` medians 54–62 ms prod, 85–113 ms dev). Profiled locally with `E2E_CPU_THROTTLE=4` (CDP `Emulation.setCPUThrottlingRate`) and temporary timestamps.
- **Before (4×, prod, ms from the click's `timeStamp`):** event dispatch 2–15 (main thread busy) · history push + recipe < 1 · React render of the piece change (sync, `useSyncExternalStore` flushes in a microtask) + wait for the next frame 20–30 · incremental compile 1–2 · second React render (of `compiled`, again ahead of the swap job's microtasks) 15–20 · flatten + post < 1. Median 50.6 prod, 74 dev. Autosave, stats, previews and history were all < 1 ms; the audition is off while playing and never added a compile.
- **Fix:** one-off edits skip the frame and the renders.
  - `setPiece(piece, { now: true })` (store): compile, post the swap, then one store update `{ piece, compiled }` (one render instead of two). `edit`, `amend`, `undo`, `redo` and `cancelGesture` use it; drags (`updateGesture`) and plain `setPiece` keep the once-per-frame compile, which skips if a `now` edit already compiled the piece.
  - `app/audio.ts` queue: with the engine created and no job running, a job starts synchronously (ordering unchanged: behind a running job it waits).
  - `AudioEngine.swap`: when no wavetables need building (any edit that doesn't change a timbre) it posts at once instead of after an `await`.
  - Audition after an inspector pick: the edit is already compiled, so it plays at the end of the task (`compiledThisTask()`) instead of waiting for the next compile; a newer edit in the same task still replaces it.
  - No memoisation needed: render cost is now after the swap.
- **After:** unthrottled clicks 0.8–1.7 ms (was 13–34). 4×, 3 runs each: prod `clickChord` 15–22, `clickWalk` 21–23, `setPiece` 10–16; dev `clickChord` 16–18, `clickWalk` 23. What is left is event dispatch while a frame or render holds the main thread.
- **Gate:** chromium-prod ≤ 50 ms median per path; chromium-dev ≤ 150 ms (React dev mode, not shipped). `E2E_CPU_THROTTLE` is an optional env for local slow-machine runs.
- **Tests:** `unit/app/edit-swap.test.ts` (swap before the store update, one update, drags per frame, no double compile), `unit/app/audio-queue.test.ts`; `audition.test.ts` step tests now flush a microtask.
- **Status:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=2` 1288/1288. E2E edit-latency, inspector, audition, app on chromium-dev and -prod 83/83.
## M4.5-27: the start is a moment, schema v2 (2026-11-21)
`Piece.start` is gone; `PIECE_VERSION` = 2. Every voice starts dormant.
- **Establishing** (`core/moments/establish.ts`; compiler `establish`): a `chord` (notes other than `current`) or `unison` at a time when no line is active brings its voices in on their notes: life `born` = `at`, `fadeIn` 0 (the DSP's minimum declick still applies), an open hold carrying the moment's `momentId` (so dots, section ownership and auditions find it). Voices on one pitch stack into one line (count, weight, members), as `initStart` did. Choices (integrator to confirm):
  - which voices: a `{ voices }` target's listed voices in listed order, else dormant voices in pool order; a chord `count` beyond the listed ones adds other free voices;
  - how many: chord `count`, else every listed voice, else one per note (pitch list length, or the chord's pitch classes); unison: listed voices, else one;
  - notes: `byVoice` + one pitch per voice gives pitch i to voice i; else voiced as for newborns, free octaves before unisons (`distinctTargets`), low to high in pool order. With no `register`: a window of 12 + 3k semitones around the middle of the voices' ranges, and `even` voices `close` there (an `even` triad over the whole range skipped the third). Unison `tonic`/`center` read the first voice's range middle.
- **Transitional from silence:** every other point moment returns early (`no-effect`, "no note is sounding; start with a chord or a unison"); a walk that starts in silence and never steps gets the same diagnostic at `finish` (one that steps after a later chord does not). Level keeps "no note is sounding". The palette (`effect.ts effectOn`) agrees: only chord, unison and key are enabled from silence. Pin from silence is also no-effect (not listed in the spec; it has nothing to move).
- **Migration** (`migrations.ts`, the single v1 → v2 step; R9-1's optional `VoiceDef.part` needs none): `start` → `startChord`: a chord at 0, id `start0` (`start1`… if taken), `pitches` in start order, first in `moments`. A start of the first pool voices in pool order with ascending pitches is a plain chord; any other gets `byVoice`, plus `{ voices }` when the voices are not the first ones in order (the three compositions have `byVoice`, all pool voices). Bad v1 starts (unknown or repeated voice, non-number pitch) still fail: "Upgrading from file format 1 failed: start[i].voice: …". Share links carry the whole JSON (version inside), so old links open through `parsePiece`; My pieces entries too; saves write v2.
- **Files on disk are v2:** the 5 originals, 3 compositions and the 5 property regressions (raw `migratePiece`, Prettier). `tests/golden/v1/` keeps tidewater-hymn and kite-day as v1 (Prettier-ignored).
- **Goldens:** `tests/golden/migration-v2.test.ts` pins an audible hash (`canonical.ts audibleJson`: the score without moment spans, harmony, diagnostics and segment moment ids) computed with the v1 compiler on main 6783fd9. All 8 examples are identical. The full hashes changed only by the opening's span (+1 span each), its harmony entry at 0 (the HUD names the opening; one note reads "A (unison)") and the opening holds' `momentId`; segment counts, lives and diagnostics are unchanged. `arrive.test.ts` PRE_REV6 hashes updated for the same reason. `generate-length` snapshot: byte-identical once the piece is written back as v1 (`hashPieceAsV1`).
- **Generate** emits `moments[0]` = the Seed chord (one note, A3, label `Seed`), keyed apart (`OPENING_KEY`) so every later id, seed and sound is unchanged.
- **New piece** (`newPiece`, `openingNote`): one chord at 0 with one note (A3, id `start0`). **Suggestions** from silence: "Start on <tonic triad>" (degrees) and "Start on <tonic>" (unison).
- **App:** Notes drawer loses "At start" and the Start column (`voices.ts` start recipes removed); shrinking the pool drops a by-voice chord's pitches with its listed voices. `chordNotes` lists a by-voice opening in voice order (editing a composition's opening keeps each voice on its own note). Hover dots include openings (`arrival-points opening`). `sectionOwner` before the first move is the opening.
- **Diagnostics registry** (coordinator): `wobble-ignored`, `note-not-sounding`, `note-back-skipped` added; `start-duplicate`/`start-out-of-range` kept as retired; `unknown-moment-type` level corrected to `info` (as emitted). `diagnostic-codes.test.ts` scans `src/core` for emitted codes.
- **Tests:** `core/establish.test.ts` (template, pitch lists, chord symbols + count, byVoice, unison, stacking, incremental, no-effect per transitional type, walk over silence, palette); `golden/migration-v2.test.ts`; schema/migration tests for v1 → v2; `e2e/start-moment.spec.ts` (New: one dot at 0:00, Delete → silence and greyed Shift/split/merge/walk/note, chord → sound, no glide). Test pieces use `withStart` (compiler-fixtures) or an opening chord; e2e specs that set `start` now unshift one. `line-artifacts` exempts establishing births (no fade-in).
- **Known nit (not fixed):** the opening's lane item falls back to `estimateSpan` (its compiled span is zero-length), so its box reserves a glide-sized width and can push a moment a few seconds later onto a new sub-row. `lane-rows` "overlapping moments stack" loads that case without an opening. Using the zero span for chord/unison (`laneItems`) did not change the count there and broke a lane unit test; left for the lane owner.
- **Gates:** lint, typecheck; `pnpm test --maxWorkers=2` 1317/1317; `pnpm test:perf` green (bench piece now 201 moments: 200 plus its opening). E2E (`--workers=1`, chromium-dev and -prod): audition, dots, note-points, octave-drag, score-select, silent-lines, snap, start-moment, app, generate, my-pieces, voices, export green after fixes; full chromium-dev suite run once, its 7 failures (specs that replaced `moments` and dropped the opening: context-moments, editor-polish, lane-rows, suggestions) fixed and green on dev and prod. Main (d14231c and later, to 72a9f74) merged before the gates.
## M4.5-28: Compose for me (2026-11-21)
- **Steps** (`core/generate.ts`): `composeSteps(seed, opts)` → `{ base, steps, sections }`; `base` is the finished piece with no moments, a step is `{ kind: 'add', moment, section }` or `{ kind: 'edit', moment, pass }` (the moment's full new form, replacing it by id). `composedPiece(c, k)` replays the first k; before the last step the end is automatic, so every prefix is an ordinary piece. `generate()`/`generateWithSections` replay all steps. Byte-identical to the old `generate()` for 200 recorded cases (`compose-steps-snapshot.json`, recorded before the split; seeds, modes, short and full lengths, 2–32 notes, densities).
- **Adjustment passes (decision).** Today's composer is a single forward pass, so the passes are honest restatements of it, not new music:
  - `register`: Drift chords are placed with harmony and timing only; after the last one, a pass gives each its register (the `GenRegister` draw is index-keyed, so moving it is exact). With 12 notes the register spans nearly the whole range, so the change is small; the pass still shows (camera back to each chord, the chord highlighted). Fewer notes show it clearly.
  - `fit` (short form, < 100 s): moments are laid out at the natural pace (unscaled times, glides, walk end), then every moment is edited to its scaled time; very visible.
- **Pace (decision)** (`app/compose-pace.ts`): a pen sweeps the piece at 7.5 s of music per second (faster for long pieces, so the sweep is ≤ 40 s); an add places its moment at the pen and draws its lines in up to the next moment's time; ≥ 250 ms per add; an edit holds 450 ms, a whole pass ≤ 3 s. A 2:30 piece builds in about 23 s (unit test: 15–25 s over 5 seeds × 3 option sets); 30 s ≈ 6.5 s, 5 min ≈ 42 s, 15 min ≈ 43 s.
- **App** (`app/compose.ts`, `ui/ComposePanel.tsx`): Menu → New: **Empty** (`new-piece`) | **Compose for me** (`compose-piece`). Compose loads an empty New piece, opens a side panel in the inspector's column (under the score on phones) with Generate's inputs (`ui/GenerateFields.tsx`, now shared with the Generate dialog; test ids `compose-*`) and starts the build from a fresh seed. The build is one editor gesture (`history.currentGesture()` added to check it is still ours), so the compose is one undo entry back to the empty piece. Any input change restarts it live; after a finished or stopped build, the restart first undoes that entry if it is still the newest and untouched (else it composes over the current piece as a new entry). Skip = finished piece + Fit; Stop keeps the prefix; an outside edit, undo or drag stops the build; loading another piece closes the panel.
- **Drawing:** `ScoreStyle.reveal` (render) clips the lines at the pen; the edited moment of a pass is highlighted (`composeView.flash`, merged with the selection highlight in the loop). The camera eases toward the pen (snaps under reduced motion) while stopped.
- **Playback:** not driven. Playing during a build plays the piece as it grows (each step is an ordinary hot-swapped edit) and the camera follows the playhead instead.
- **Tests:** `unit/core/compose-steps.test.ts` (200-case replay, order, passes, every prefix parses and compiles without errors, restart), `unit/app/compose.test.ts` (pace, moments over time, Skip, Stop + one undo/redo, mid-build and after-finish restarts as one entry, outside edit stops), `unit/render/selection.test.ts` (reveal clip), `e2e/compose.spec.ts` (over time, Skip + one undo, Stop plays, seed change restarts).
- **Gates:** lint, typecheck; `pnpm test --maxWorkers=2` 1338/1338. E2E (`--workers=1`, chromium-dev and -prod): compose, generate, start-moment, my-pieces, inspector, render 59 passed (render's 1080p budget runs on dev only). `test:perf` not run: no compiler or DSP change (generate.ts is not on the compile path; draw.ts gains one optional clip).

An empty span on any other moment still shows the intended glide. The 27 attempt failed for two reasons: it also changed no-op chords (the unit test), and `lane-rows`' `setWindow` still mapped the slider to [2 s, duration], not to the open canvas, so "20 s" was ~168 s and the labels really overlapped. Helper fixed; the sub-row test loads the opening again and asserts it has no bar and shares stk001's sub-row (fails without the fix: bar 179 px).
- **`mouse-only.spec`** (audit gap 6): New → Empty, palette drag (chord), inspector chord click, ruler-click seek + palette click (split), palette drag (walk), split dot drag, loop band drag, Play (stays in the loop), Export WAV (header checked); undo walks back through each step to the opening, redo forward; then, playing, inspector chord and walk-chip clicks timed to the worklet swap. Click → swap median ~1 ms (dev and prod). The latency hooks moved to `tests/e2e/latency-hook.ts` (shared with `edit-latency.spec`).
- **Findings (not fixed):** undo and redo have no button or menu item, so mouse-only use cannot undo; a chord added to the empty template sounds one note ("D (unison)") because the opening has one note; the loop region is view state outside undo, and a band drag stores unrounded times.
