## Arrival bloom (dsp, 2026-11-21)
- **Problem:** the moment-end pop (entry above) is the articulation strike: glass +3.7 dB in 12 ms on every voice at once.
- **Approach: DSP onset shaping, no migration.** Pieces embed their timbres, so only the engine reaches them all; the files keep their values.
  - **Bloom attack** (`timbreParams`): a soft strike lasts at least `BLOOM_ATTACK`·√amount = 50 ms·√amount, besides the 12 ms floor. Curvature ∝ amount ÷ attack², so this caps it near 1.5 % per block for any amount. Glass 4 → 30 ms, organ 3 → 17 ms, reed 20 → 22 ms; choir and wobble unchanged.
  - **Coincident strikes share one bloom** (`computeStrikeShares`, called from `Graph.prepare` beside the unison warps): each arrival rises to 1/√k of its amount, with k = 1 + a raised-cosine count of the other voices arriving within 40 ms. k coherent strikes then sum like √k incoherent ones. A 4-voice glass chord blooms +2.0 dB, not +3.7 dB; staggered arrivals (≥ 40 ms apart) strike in full. Only the strike's height changes: the held level stays 1 − amount, so balance is untouched. The envelope stays continuous when the shares of consecutive arrivals differ, since the attack starts from the previous strike's height.
  - **`crisp`** (new optional `Timbre.articulation.crisp`; absent means amount ≥ 0.5): keeps the 12 ms floor and is never shared. Pluck (0.9) is crisp, so its owner-approved 12 ms attack is unchanged. Organ's 12 ms key click measured 0.031, over the bar, so it blooms over 17 ms.
- **Deviation (PLAN §8, `articulation(t)`):** the effective attack can be longer than the timbre's, and chord strikes are lower. Both are noted in the type's doc comment. The owner may want to hear it.
- **Metric** (block-rate second difference of a voice's amplitude ÷ the strike's peak; the clicks test's "12 ms step"). For a 4-voice chord at stagger 0, before → after: glass 0.086 → 0.0096, organ 0.031 → 0.0096, reed 0.019 → 0.0088, wobble 0.0089 → 0.0049, choir 0.0006 → 0.0003, sine 0.0001, pluck 0.222 → 0.222. Solo arrivals after: ≤ 0.018 (organ's is its 80 ms decay corner). In clicks.test's ends piece, glass went from 0.13 to 0.011. In Kite Day's 63 arrivals, glass went from 0.089 to 0.018 and organ from 0.032 to 0.020.
- **For R9-3b (pulse strikes):** the envelope is (amount, attack, decay, crisp) from `TimbreParams` (`artAttack` is already the effective attack, `artCrisp` is 0/1) plus a per-strike start time and height: `VoiceData.arrive`/`share` and `prevArrive`/`prevShare` per segment. A pulse hit can set its own time and height (for example, its accent). Use `crisp: true` on a timbre for crisp pulse hits. If pulse hits should never be shared, pass share 1.
- **Known:** the attack's start value assumes the previous strike's attack began from 0 (as before). Arrivals under ~30 ms apart can therefore step slightly. MIDI doesn't read articulation.
- **Tests:** `tests/dsp/arrival-bloom.test.ts` checks each preset in a chord and solo, the chord sum, pluck > 0.1, the attack rule, the `crisp` override, the shares (0.5 for 4 together, 1 when staggered, smooth in between), and that the held level doesn't change. In clicks.test, glass arrivals must now be < 0.03 and pluck is the positive control. No render hashes are pinned (goldens hash the compiled score, which is unchanged).
- **Kite Day A/B** (24-bit, 20 s): `/private/tmp/claude-501/pop/kite-day-before.wav` (base 2311686) and `kite-day-after.wav`. They differ only from each strike onward, decaying with it. Total RMS −0.05 dB.
- **Gates:** `test:perf` at load ~9–16: no-GC 0 scavenges in both runs, RTF 17×. `pnpm test` 1492/1492. lint and typecheck clean.
## R9-9: land at the marker, snap to beats (app, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §11.3 "Land on the beat", "Snap to beats", "Snapping and bars", "Every dot is draggable".
- **Land:** inspector Transition "Land at the marker" (`landGlide` writes `glide.land` only when it differs from the piece default; shows the effective value; a note says where the glide starts, from core `landing`). The lane's bars already ended at the marker from the compiled `momentSpans`; what was missing: `estimateSpan` (stale/muted/empty spans, ghosts) now uses `landing()`; `LaneItem.land`; tooltip "Notes arrive on the marker; the glide starts at …", aria-label; for a landing bar the end handle sits on the bar's **start** and dragging it left lengthens the glide (the end stays; `endDragSpan`/`endDragDelta`). An end-drag preview is now computed from the bar at the press (the old `origEnd` logic, kept relative so the live recompile doesn't double it).
- **Snap to beats:** `view.snapBeats?: boolean` (types, `ViewSchema`, docs/file-format.md regenerated). Absent = on when `gridBpm` is set; stored only when off. View state like `loop`: set with `setSnapBeats`, not undone, kept across `setPiece`. `editor/snap.ts`: `beatStep(view)`, `SnapSet.grid`; `snapTime` takes the nearer of the nearest beat (any distance) and a note-change point within 8 px (ties: the beat); a walk's sibling steps still come first; Alt = free. Wired into lane moment drags, palette drops (Alt now honoured), dot drags (`dotSnaps`), note points added on the score, and "Next" (`suggestionTime`: next beat at or after the last end). Not wired: ruler (loop edges, end handle), arrow-key nudges (still by beat whenever there is a tempo), "Add" at the playhead (as specified).
- **Deviations / notes:** existing specs that set `gridBpm: 60` and expect free times now set `snapBeats: false` (lane, note-points); note-points' drag is shorter because the new button gave the score a little less height at 1280×860.
- **Tests:** unit: lane.test (land estimate vs compile, end-drag geometry), snap.test (beatStep, nearest/next beat, priorities, Alt, dotSnaps), suggestions.test, inspector-fields.test (landGlide), schema.test (snapBeats). e2e: land.spec.ts, snap-beats.spec.ts (chromium dev).
## R9-4: the `tune` moment (core, 2026-11-21)
PLAN rev 9 `tune`: types (`TuneMoment`, `TuneNote`), schema (`notes` default `[]`, `len` > 0; no version bump: older builds keep unknown moments), compile, incremental, palette check, transposition, app wiring (Inspector "Tune", `actions` label, `defaultMoment`; not in the palette). No flat-layout or DSP change: a tune is ordinary segments.
- **Compile** (`compile.ts onTune`, `moments/tune.ts`): note k starts at s_k = at + Σ len before it. Each note is its own moment-class event (`SUB_TUNE`, state on an immutable `TuneStep`, saved in checkpoints like note returns) and runs through `applyPointMoment` as a pin-like unordered move of the whole line (`countsLines` includes tune, so a merged line moves whole). Overrides and the HUD harmony are skipped for its notes.
- **Glide** (`momentGlide`): `TUNE_GLIDE` (length 0.1, stagger 0, jitter 0) sits between the piece default and the moment's own glide; used by compile, `decisionTime` and the effect env. Without land, the glide starts at s_k and is capped at len_k. With `land`, note k arrives at s_k and its glide starts no earlier than s_{k−1} (`landing(at, glide, floor)`, new `floor` argument, default 0).
- **Line:** the first of the target in pitch order (committed destinations, so a staggered newborn heading lowest counts; its move then starts at its birth). Later notes follow that line through merges (`tuneLine`: Dying → `into`; closed → the active line that has it in `members`); else the first of the target again; else `tune-lost` (info) and the rest is skipped.

**Decisions (PLAN is silent):**
- "First in pitch order" = lowest (rank 0), as `rank` selectors count.
- Not establishing: from silence, or with no targeted line, `no-effect`. Also `no-effect` for an empty tune ("the tune has no notes") and when no note moved the line (raised at the last note). `tune-invalid` (error): notes not a list, a non-finite pitch or len ≤ 0.
- Merges: a tune note onto a resting line of its part merges as any arrival (the line there first survives and plays on; another part's line never merges, R9-1 `onJoin`). Tunes and walks: walks never step a line while a tune plays on it (`VoiceRun.tuneUntil`, checkpointed, NUMS 20 → 21; passed to the survivor of a join). Other moments may still move the line; the next note takes it back.
- A repeated pitch is a hold: no move, so no articulation re-strike (for R9-3b if wanted). Its whole region is its span and counts as activity.
- `readState` treats later tune segments like walk steps (decided at their own start). `hasFixedNotes`/`remapMoment`: tune notes are fixed notes and move with a key change (not folded: the line is chosen at compile time).

**For R9-7/8:** `lane.ts estimateSpan` and the inspector's land note still merge the piece glide, not `momentGlide`, for tunes. Palette/inspector editor/dots not done.
**Tests:** `tests/unit/core/tune.test.ts` (timing, holding, crossing, span/activity, trace, harmony, glide length/cap/defaults, land + floor, land from silence, targeting by part/voices, later moments, no targets, silence, other-part no-merge, no-parts merge and carry-on, own-part merge, walks leave the line alone (also after a join), pulse on the line, range fold, empty/no-effect/invalid, palette check vs compiler, incremental = full over six edits and repeat compiles, transposition, schema). `tune` in `momentArb` (weight 2); new property "tune notes" (one line, unordered, moves at the note to its folded pitch; also run on the regressions). Its first counterexample (2611/112: the tune's lowest line was a land split's newborn not born yet, so its move starts at the birth) was the property's mistake, fixed there and pinned as `regressions/tune-newborn-seed2611.json`. Crossing properties already skip unordered plans and joins are checked per part, so no exemptions were needed. editor.test default moments include tune; e2e inspector "Tune". Goldens unchanged.
**Found on the way:** the missing `SUB_TUNE` snapshot case lost tune notes on resume (caught by the unit test, fixed before commit). Sweep failures not involving tunes (left open): chord-count "spaced pieces" crossing seed 412527772/111 (its generator has no tunes); distinct-notes "with parts" seed 1382238733 (shrunk piece: unison, chord count 4, merge, pulse; no tune).
**Sweeps:** 3 × `PROP_SEED=random PROP_RUNS=1000` over `tests/property/core` green after the above. **Gates:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=2` 1544/1544 (after the schema message fix), `test:perf` 5/5 (load ~6), e2e:quick inspector Tune/Pulse (chromium).
## R9-7: pulse and tune editing UI (app, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §6.2 `pulse`/`tune`, §11.3. App only; no core, dsp or render change.
- **Palette:** `pulse` (┇, key R) after walk, `tune` (♪, key E) after note. Core's `EFFECT_MOMENT_TYPES` still omits them, so `paletteEffects` checks them one by one with `momentEffectAt` (greyed with core's reason: from silence, no sounding line, empty tune). Drop, drag preview and hover ghost are the generic paths; the ghost draws the bar art too (`paintArt`). Palette buttons are 4 px narrower so 13 fit one row at 1280 px (otherwise the palette wraps and the lanes lose a row's height; lane/lane-rows specs caught it).
- **Lane** (`editor/lane.ts`): `endEditOf` pulse → `until`, tune → `notes` (the end handle sets the last note's length, `tune.ts tuneEndDrag`). `estimateSpan`: pulse [at, until]; tune [at, at + Σlen], led in by `momentGlide` (0.1 s) with land. `landsAtMarker` is false for both (the bar is the region; land acts per tune note). `LaneItem.art`: a pulse's pattern cycle as an SVG mask repeated every cycle (`pulse.ts patternSvg`, `cycleShare`), a tune's notes as dashes by time and pitch, both in % of the bar. A region's **end drag now snaps** like a move (beats, note changes within 8 px; Alt free); this includes walks (before: free). Undo labels "Change region" / "Change last note length". Lane moves carry a pulse's `until` (`ops.shifted`); the inspector's Time field moves a walk's or pulse's whole region (`atPatch`; before, a walk's `until` stayed put).
- **Pulse inspector** (`PulseFields.tsx`): Region (length, like walk), Step as beat chips (1/4, 1/8., 1/8, 1/8 trip., 1/16; a beat is a quarter) when the piece has a tempo, plus seconds always; Pattern as a row of slider cells (press or drag sets the accent from the height, one undo entry; ↑/↓ 5 %, Shift/Page 25 %, Home rest, End full, ←/→ move); + step / − step (1..32); Decay, Floor (%), Mode chips, "Re-strike each hit". No Transition section; Seed only in Random mode.
- **Tune inspector:** Transition shows Glide (the tune's 0.1 s default through `TUNE_GLIDE`), Curve and Land (note "Each note arrives on its start"); no stagger, jitter, arrive, order or seed. Notes list (`TuneFields.tsx`): start, name in the key at that time, length (beats with `gridBpm`, else seconds; `CompactNumber`, generalised from `WeightBox`: type, ↑/↓ ¼ beat or 0.05 s, scrub), ▲/▼ by a scale step (sounds the note), × remove, "+ Add note" (a scale step above the last, same length; an empty tune starts on the lowest sounding note).
- **Defaults:** a new pulse is eighths for two bars with a tempo (else 0.5 s for 4 s), pattern [1, 0.5, 0.75, 0.5].
- **Needs the integrator (core):** `glides()` counts `pulse`, so with `defaults.glide.land` a pulse is queued at its lead-in (`at − length − stagger`) and claims voices early. Probably `glides` should exclude `pulse`. `EFFECT_MOMENT_TYPES` can list pulse and tune now. Seen in passing: a silent piece's suggestions render two "Start on C" with one React key (pre-existing warning).
- **Tests:** unit `tests/unit/app/pulse-tune.test.ts` (tune/pulse helpers, spans vs compile, end drags, bar art, region moves, defaults, palette greying, file and share round trip); shortcuts/editor/palette counts. e2e `pulse-ui.spec.ts`, `tune-ui.spec.ts` (chromium-dev); reran palette, lane, lane-dense, lane-preview, lane-rows, inspector, context-moments, snap-beats, walk-steps, land: green. Screenshots in /private/tmp/claude-501/r9-7/. Gates: lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=2` 1565/1565.
## Seeds-4: property seeds after R9-4 (core, 2026-11-21)
| Seed | Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| distinct-notes "with parts" 1382238733 (from R9-4) | Every non-note moment cancels its targets' pending moves before planning. A merge with nothing to merge (`merge-nothing`, null plan) still cancelled a chord's staggered moves: two lines left on 42, the chord's newborn stranded mid-glide at 44.6. | **Compiler** (`applyPointMoment`): a moment that plans nothing (null plan: no-op or invalid) restores the moves it cancelled, as a note already did. Such a merge now also reports `no-effect`. Regression `distinct-noop-merge-seed1382238733.json` + explicit check. |
| chord-count "spaced pieces" 412527772/111 (from R9-4) | The planner checked adjacent pairs only. v3/v4 forked from v1 while v2 (between them in target order) was unborn, so no pair saw them fall under v1 (still on its steep expo) and cross back. | **Compiler** (`plan.ts`): also checks pairs across lines not born at T (this moment's newborns, waiting earlier newborns), after the adjacent pairs in each pass. Regression `count-unborn-sibling-seed412527772.json` + explicit check. |
| incremental "random edit sequence" 2119968460/513 (new, sweep; fails on main) | A land spread (index 4) decides at 10.8, its lead-in start, before a key at 10.8 (index 6), but reads that key. Replacing the key resumed from the checkpoint between them. | **Compiler** (`incremental.ts firstChange`): the key rule takes land moments deciding at or before the key's time and bounds the resume by their index too. Regression `regressions/incremental/key-tie-seed2119968460.json` (before/after pieces; a subfolder, so the compiler regressions loop skips it) + explicit check. |
| compiler "no crossings within the targeted set" 1791964326/772 (new, sweep; fails on main) | A note's return forks v12 from the tune's line v4; the tune's next note (decided later, before v12's birth) turns v4 down, so v12 starts with its downward speed and dips under v8. | **Property:** a later moment taking over a newborn's parent before its birth ends the plan's guarantee for it, like a takeover of the line itself. Regression `crossing-parent-takeover-seed1791964326.json` + explicit check. |

**Goldens:** seven-doors re-pinned (golden, `arrive.test.ts`, `migration-v2.test.ts`), from the planner fix: at `TVyKyp` a newborn (v6) is born on the parent's pitch ahead of one (v11) forking later above it, across four unborn siblings; it now takes v11's timing and forks 35 ms earlier. Diagnostics unchanged; other examples unchanged. The merge and incremental fixes change no golden.
**Sweeps:** 10 × `PROP_SEED=random PROP_RUNS=1000` over `tests/property`: the two new failures above in sweeps 4 and 7, fixed; the last three (after all fixes) green, 64/64. **Gates:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=2` 1552/1552, `test:perf` 5/5 (load ~18).
## Merges and splits crossfade (core + dsp + render, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §11.3 "Merges and splits crossfade", "Weight", "Thickness transitions".
- **One function, carried in the score.** `core/ease.ts` `crossfade(u)` (smootherstep, clamped) and `crossfadeLength(glide)` = max(`CROSSFADE_MIN` 0.1 s, ⅓ of the glide). `ease` is a pure leaf, so the engine imports it by value. The compiler writes every window into the compiled score: a merging line's life ends at the meeting with `fadeOut` = window and `crossOut`; a fork's life has `fadeIn` = window and `crossIn`; the merged line or parent gets a `cross` weight change (`lead` = how long before its `t` the ramp starts: a merge's ends at the meeting). Sound: dying power w·X(v), newborn w·X(u), weight ramps along X, so the sum is constant. Drawing (`render/strands.ts`): bundles are read from those lives and weight changes, and `strandMix` is X(u) (merge) or 1 − X(u) (split) over exactly the compiled window. The old pixel windows (32 px minimum) are gone.
- **Windows.** Merge (coincidence join, merge moment, `count` death): ends where the lines meet; length from the longest approach among them, clipped to both lives. Split: starts at the fork's birth; length from its departure glide. With under 10 ms of room (a survivor born at the meeting) the merge hands over after the meeting as before (`passWeight`; HANDOVER for joins, the death's own fade otherwise).
- **Deviation:** merge moments and `count` deaths crossfade too (the drawing already ended them at the meeting), so `merge.fade` and a count death's glide-length fade now apply only when the line has no survivor. The inspector's merge Fade field does little now; worth hiding or relabelling (app, not done).
- **Weight timeline is additive** (`WeightChange` doc): change k adds w_k − w_(k−1) along its own ramp, so staggered forks of one parent overlap exactly. Overlapping linear ramps used to restart from the current value; now they add (same end values). `query.weightAt` and the engine (`segment.ts` `weightRamps`/`weightRampValue`: start-sorted ramps with a back index and base, allocation-free) compute the same sum.
- **Flat layout v5** (reshaped, noted): `LIFE_CROSS` flags, `LIFE_STRIDE` 5; `WEIGHT_LEAD`, `WEIGHT_CROSS`, `WEIGHT_STRIDE` 5. `computeLife` uses √X amplitude and X power for crossfade fades (`powTick` now carries power for them, so g_n stays put). `VoiceData.probe` keeps smootherstep (it only weights a starting voice's say in its group's phase; 0 at birth either way).
- **Group gain h step (pop bisect):** the crossfade alone did not remove it (onset step 0.305 → 0.305). Cause: capture switched on in one block when a voice's unison warp started, but the warp zone starts where the pair beats at 12 Hz, inside the 20 Hz h edge, so the pair entered h at full weight; out of phase, h jumped. Fix (`graph.ts`): capture fades in and out over `CAPTURE_RAMP` = 30 ms (smoothstep) per voice. Once in a group h still follows beats, by design.
- **Numbers** (`tests/dsp/crossfade.test.ts`; before = 676fbdb):
  - Lines' summed power Σ life²·w through 3 forks, 2 joins and a merge moment: max deviation 0.71 dB before (smootherstep fade against a linear ramp), 4.8e-15 dB after. Bound 0.01 dB (it is exact by construction; the bound only admits rounding). Compiled score: < 1e-9 dB.
  - h onset step per block (2.7 ms): clicks' merge piece 0.305 → 0.017; crossfade piece 0.162 → 0.015. Bound 0.05 (a full 40 % swing then takes ≥ 50 ms).
  - Clicks at crossfade starts and ends: worst 2.24 vs steady 2.95 (bound 2×). `clicks.test.ts` and `arrival-bloom.test.ts` green.
- **Other readers:** `effect.ts`, `query.linesAt`: a crossfading line counts until its meeting. MIDI keeps the hand-over: crossfades are written as ≤ 0.1 s fades at the meeting (CC11). Incremental restore keeps `crossIn`.
- **Goldens:** the five originals re-pinned (`examples.golden.json`, `arrive.test.ts`, `migration-v2.test.ts`): segments −1 to −3 per voice (no hold after a meeting) and durations −0.1 s (the last hand-over); lives, last pitches and diagnostics unchanged. Compositions unchanged: Kite Day has no merges or splits, its render is bit-identical.
- **Tests changed for the new semantics:** chord-count (count deaths), compiler-moments (merge), chord-count-lines (no riding after the meeting), property-seeds-29 (/984 lives count dropped, /907 dies at 5, /608 checks the no-room fallback), chord-count property (follower), `line-artifacts.ts` (a crossfaded death is judged at its meeting), MIDI CC11 expectation, strands fixture. New: `tests/unit/core/crossfade.test.ts`, `tests/dsp/crossfade.test.ts`, strands "drawn width follows the crossfade", shared `tests/unit/core/crossfade-fixtures.ts`.
- **Files for the owner** (`/private/tmp/claude-501/crossfade/`): `kite-day-{before,after}.wav` (identical), `tidewater-hymn-{before,after}.wav` (100 s; differs from 10.5 s, its splits and unison), `merge-split-sine-{before,after}.wav` (the test piece), `merge-split-drawn.png`, `merge-closeup.png`, `split-closeup.png` (headless chromium), `measure-{before,after}.json`.
- **Merged with R9-4:** `onJoin` keeps the crossfade and the tune's `tuneUntil` hand-on; tune landings go through `onJoin`, so they crossfade too (0.1 s, the note's glide). Tune changed no flat layout (v5 stands); NUMS 21 and `crossIn` restore coexist. New: `tuneMergePiece` (two tune merges) in the dsp power/h tests (3.9e-15 dB; h onset 0.020) and in crossfade.test (compiled power, incremental = full over edits). Goldens unchanged by the merge.
- **Merged with R9-7 and Seeds-4:** seven-doors re-pinned for both changes together (golden, `arrive.test.ts`, `migration-v2.test.ts`); the other four keep the crossfade hashes, so the two changes do not interact elsewhere. Open: one sweep (300 runs, random seed) failed compiler "note moments" (ordered plan's intents out of order); the seed was not captured and 15 × 2000 targeted runs, 15 × 300 of the file and 6 full sweeps since are green.

## R9-8: tune dots and typed notes (app, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §6.2 `tune`. App only; no core, dsp or render change.
- **Dots** (`editor/tune.ts tuneDots`, type `tune`, `ArrivalDot.note` = index): one per note at its start and stored pitch; voice = the line the tune moved for that note. Shown for a selected tune, on hover and in the marquee (`allArrivals` uses them instead of the tune's landings). Dragging goes through the shared dot path (`dragDots` → `moveDots` → `tunePatch`): vertically the pitch (key snap, Alt chromatic, Shift octaves), sideways the start (`moveTuneStarts`: the note's end stays, so the note before it and the note change length; note 0 moves `at`; all notes of a tune moving shift it; clamped to `MIN_TUNE_LEN` and ≥ 0); snaps via `dotSnaps` (beats, other moments' changes; Alt free). Undo label "Move tune note". `isDot` tells tune dots apart by `note`.
- **Keyboard:** tune dots are tab stops (the only dots that are) with aria-labels; a click or drag focuses the dot. Focused: ↑/↓ a scale step (Alt: a semitone), ←/→ the start by a beat (`tuneBeat`, i.e. `gridBpm`, regardless of Snap to beats) or 0.1 s; Shift+↑/↓ stays the document's octave move of the selected dots. `stepTuneDot`/`nudgeTuneDot` in actions.
- **Dot ↔ row:** the inspector rows read the dot selection (`li.on`, `data-picked`, scrolled into view); the row's start + name is a pick button that selects that note's dot (again: clears).
- **Typed notes** (`TextField` "Type", `parseTuneText`/`formatTuneText`): `F#5 2, E5 1, D5 1`; beats with a tempo, else seconds; flats, ♯/♭, cents (`C4+25`), decimals; a missing length repeats the previous one; empty text = empty tune. Names only (a bare number is refused: `E5, 1` would otherwise read 1 as MIDI 1). Inline errors name the note; nothing changes until valid; Enter or blur commits ("Type tune notes"). Shown text is spelled in the key at each note (C major shows Bb4 as A#4). A length typed as shown keeps its exact value, so committing unchanged text is a no-op.
- **Merge Fade:** relabelled "Fade (when no line survives)" (`insp-merge-fade`). The compiled score does not say whether a merge left a dying line without a survivor (that is decided per part inside the compile), so hiding the field would need a core export; the label was simpler.
- **Tests:** unit `tune-dots.test.ts` (dots, selection identity, start moves and clamps, moveDots incl. group drags, parser/formatter: beats and seconds, flats, decimals, cents, errors, round trip in three keys). e2e `tune-dots.spec.ts` (drag pitch/beats/Alt/first note, dot↔row, arrow keys, typed beats/seconds/errors/blur/undo/share link, merge label). Reran tune-ui, dots, note-points, inspector, snap-beats, score-select, walk-steps (chromium-dev, own ports): green. Screenshots in /private/tmp/claude-501/r9-8/. Gates: lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=2` 1587/1587.
## R9-3b: pulse in sound and MIDI (dsp + midi, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §6.2 `pulse`, §12 CC11. Hits were silent after R9-3a.
- **Engine** (`dsp/voice.ts`): the pulse gain multiplies the level in `render` (so it multiplies life fades, crossfade weights and level moments, and stays out of g_n like level). Core's envelope jumps; the voice rises from the envelope it was on (`pulseFrom`, the last record at an earlier time) to the record's level over an onset attack A (smootherstep), holds the accent at t + A, then falls by core's cubic compressed into decay − A, so onset, peak and floor time land where core puts them. A follows the bloom: crisp timbres `MIN_ARTICULATION_ATTACK` (12 ms), soft ones ≥ `BLOOM_ATTACK`·√Δ (Δ = jump size, ≤ 50 ms), capped by the next record (continuity) and decay/2; a record at a birth applies at once. Precomputed in `preparePulses` (`fromFlat`).
- **Strikes:** render now reads one strike list (`buildStrikes`: segment arrivals + hits with `strike`), cursor `strikeCur`. Pulse strikes have share 1 and use the timbre's own `crisp`/attack; an arrival and a hit at the same time strike once at the larger share. Non-pulse renders are bit-identical to main (Kite Day, Tidewater, Clockwork Heron, 30 s).
- **MIDI CC11** (option): life × level × pulse (core `pulseGainAt`) × √(weight / the life's peak weight). Crossfades fade over their whole window as the engine plays them (√X); the `HANDOVER` clamp is gone. **Deviation:** with CC11 on, notes strike at the velocity of their life's peak weight (CC11 carries the rest), since velocity can't follow a merge's swell; without CC11 velocity is unchanged. **Sparsity:** write when the value moved ≥ 2 steps (1.6 %), at most every 5 ms (200/s, PLAN's glide bend rate), jumps on their tick, exact value once a change settles. Error ≤ 1.5 steps plus ≤ 5 ms lag.
- **Land fix (core):** `glides()` excludes `pulse` (with `defaults.glide.land` a pulse started at its lead-in). Unit test in `pulse.test.ts` (red before).
- **Numbers** (`tests/dsp/pulse.test.ts`, sine gate unless noted): onsets ≤ 0.021 ms after the hit (1 ms bound); per-hit peak / accent within 0.07 %; floor 0 exactly silent from t + decay, floor 0.2 within 0.016 %. Click detector at onsets vs steady: sine 2.79 / 2.67, glass 4.31 / 4.29 (bound 2×); pluck 4.82 / 4.39. Envelope rise (block second difference / peak): sine 0.026, glass 0.029 (bound 0.03), pluck 0.20 (crisp). Pluck strikes: every hit's peak within 0.8 %; `strike: false` 0.10. Pulse × crossfade × level vs the bare piece: 1e-15 (unlocked; a unison group's h couples members, as for level). Live worklet (stubbed globals) = offline, bit-identical; Σ stems − mix −153 / −146 dBFS (`pulse-match.test.ts`). MIDI: gate 34 events per hit (42 % of every 7-bit change), chord piece 25.5 (35 %); crossfade piece's MIDI power within 0.10 dB of the engine's.
- **Known:** a hit's peak is a corner (slope 0 → −3·accent/(decay − A)), as core's spec implies; it is not where the envelope metric peaks. Hits closer than A cut A short.
- **Workloads:** `withPulses` (fixtures/scores) puts pulses on every other voice of the RTF and no-GC runs.
- **Needs the integrator (app):** the export dialog's CC11 label still says "life and level"; now also pulse and crossfade weight.
- **Demo** (`tests/unit/core/pulse-fixtures.ts pulseDemoPiece`, 96 bpm, glass pad gated in eighths, pluck up-down sixteenths over gliding chords, organ bass on 1 and 3): `/private/tmp/claude-501/r9-3b/pulse-demo.wav` (27.8 s, peak −1.8 dBFS, no limiting), `pulse-chord-glass.wav` (the click test's piece).
- **Gates:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=2` 1631/1631, `test:perf` 5/5 at load ~2 (RTF 19.4× with pulses, no-GC 0 scavenges both runs, compile median 26 ms), e2e:quick `export.spec` 9/9 (chromium-dev).

- **Note-moments hunt (CC11 label builder):** about 70 random-seed sweeps x 2000 runs (~140k runs; PROP_SEED=random, -t "note moments") all green; failure not reproduced.

## M5 share links (app + core, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §11.4 "Share links", §15 M5; docs/owner-decisions.md "Share links (M5)".
- **v2 links** (`app/share.ts`): `#p=2.` + base64url(deflate-raw with a preset dictionary). The body is the piece with defaults stripped, built-in timbres equal to the preset written as `1`, voice ids `v<i+1>` omitted, moment ids omitted when they count up from the previous one (`kd0041` → `kd0042`), moment `at` as integer-ms steps from the previous moment, and tune notes as flat `[pitch, len, …]`. Every encode checks that the link parses back to the piece; otherwise it writes a v1 link (stripped JSON, or the whole piece). Unmarked links are v1 and open as before ('.' is not base64url, so the marker is unambiguous).
- **Dictionary** (`app/share-dict.ts`, ~3.6 kB of JSON fragments): part of the format, never edit; a better one needs `3.`. Needs fflate (already a dependency, now loaded on demand by share.ts), since CompressionStream takes no dictionary. fflate deflates 4–8 % worse than zlib; still fits. v2 decode is bounded (1 KB input steps, 8 MB output).
- **Default stripping** (`core/schema.ts stripDefaults`, schema-driven): a field is dropped when the schema fills the same value for it, at any level (piece, meta, glide, voices, moments by type, timbres, mix, view, end; a whole default pool too); unknown fields are kept; keys come out in `serializePiece` order, so equal pieces give the same link. `jsonEqual` exported.
- **Canonical numbers** (`core/canonical.ts`): times 1 ms; tune `len`, pulse `step`, walk `grid` 1 µs (they accumulate: night-ferry's 0.357143 s step would drift 57 ms at 1 ms); levels, gains, pan, sends, accents, weights, jitter, density 3 significant digits; other known numbers (pitches, factors, ticks, béziers, Hz) 12 digits. Only listed fields: unknown fields, timbres and the mix (no editor yet) are kept as written. Applied silently in `parsePiece` and in the store's `loadPiece` and `setPiece` (copy-on-write, so unchanged moments keep identity for the incremental compiler). **Deviation:** the integrator asked for canonicalising where pieces are created and edited; I did it in `setPiece`/`loadPiece` instead of in each editor recipe, so the editor (other builder) is untouched and every path is covered.
- **Goldens re-pinned:** lanterns (float noise: 133.74599999999998 → 133.746) and the three compositions on main (times at 0.1 ms → 1 ms, gains such as 1.1199999999999999). Compiled vs as-written: identical shape, largest change 0.4 ms in time and 0.005 in linear gain (≤ 0.04 dB); `canonical.test.ts` checks every example stays within that. The other four originals are bit-identical. Updated `examples.golden.json`, `migration-v2.test.ts`, `arrive.test.ts`.
- **UI:** the menu has "Share link…" (`ui/ShareDialog.tsx`): the link in a read-only field with its length and Copy; over 2,000 characters it warns that chat apps cut long links and offers Save as file. Built-in timbres are restored from the current presets on decode, so a retuned preset changes how old links sound (as §11.4 implies).
- **Link lengths** (full URL on baphaelman.github.io/algorhythms/; before = v1 of the parsed piece): clockwork-heron 2,096 → 1,037; lanterns 2,102 → 1,066; seven-doors 2,194 → 1,097; cartographers 2,144 → 1,085; tidewater 2,066 → 1,008; rev9 harbour-song 3,180 → 1,694, kite-day 2,668 → 1,274, night-ferry 3,346 → 1,622. The pre-rewrite compositions still on main stay over (harbour 9,047 → 7,752, kite 7,083 → 5,477, night-ferry 23,926 → 19,844).
- **Tests:** unit `share.test.ts` (v1 kept, v2 round trip and same link back, old links, zlib interop with the dictionary, packing rules, unknown fields, non-canonical input), `share-length.test.ts` (every example ≤ 2,000 and round-trips; rev9 fixtures copied into `tests/unit/app/fixtures/compositions-rev9/`; on-disk compositions are checked once they equal their rewrite), `core/strip-defaults.test.ts` (each default stripped and restored), `core/canonical.test.ts`; property `property/app/share.property.test.ts` (pieceArb with float noise: decode(encode(p)) = canonical p, encode(decode(link)) = link; rounding idempotent). e2e `share.spec.ts` (dialog link opens the same piece and re-encodes to the same link; long link warns and saves a file); `app.spec.ts` expects the `2.` marker.
## M5 desktop: piano ruler, multi-select, copy and paste, Freeze (app, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §11.3, §15 M5. App, plus one optional render field (`ScoreStyle.axisInset`: the A-line labels start after the ruler).
- **Audit (what M4/M4.5 already had):** lane Shift-click toggle, drag of the whole selection, Alt-drag duplicate, Delete/Backspace and ←/→ nudge of the selection (one undo entry each), Escape; score click selects a line's moment (Shift toggles), background-drag marquee selects dots and their moments, group dot drags; chord freeze on the first note edit and walk freeze on the first step edit, each with Unfreeze; a multi-selection inspector (Mute all, Delete). **Missing, now built:** ⌘/Ctrl-click toggling (lane, score lines, dots), a lane marquee, select all, copy/cut/paste, an explicit Freeze, the piano ruler.
- **Multi-select** (`editor/selection.ts`, Lane): Shift-drag on the lane background draws a marquee (a plain drag still pans); it selects moments whose marker, label or bar it touches, replacing the selection (⌘/Ctrl+Shift adds), then focuses the first so ←/→ and Delete act on the group. ⌘/Ctrl+A selects every moment (not the piece key). Announced in the lane's live region (`announce.ts`).
- **Copy/cut/paste** (`editor/clipboard.ts`, `clipboard.ts`): clip = JSON `{ format: 'algorhythms-moments', version: 1, moments }`, times relative to the earliest, seeds made explicit (as Alt-drag duplicates). Written through the `copy`/`cut` event (`text/plain`) and kept in-app; `paste` reads the event's text, ignores non-clip text, and uses the in-app clip when the clipboard gives nothing. If an engine fires no clipboard event for the shortcut, a timeout fallback runs (copy: `navigator.clipboard.writeText`, paste: in-app). Paste puts the earliest at the playhead (nearest beat with Snap to beats), new ids, schema-checked (`parsePiece`; refused with a message if invalid), selected, one undo entry ("Paste moments"); cut is "Cut moments". Text fields, page text selections and open dialogs keep native behaviour.
- **Freeze** (`editor/freeze.ts`, inspector button, "Freeze N" for several): chord (`freezeChord` + `frozenPatch`, Unfreeze as before), split (its landed pitches as a `pitches` NoteSet, traced like `freezeChord`), walk (`freezeWalk` → `fixed`, Unfreeze as before); explicit seed; one undo entry. Other types have nothing to bake, so no button. **Gap:** a frozen split has no Unfreeze (undo only); it would need `frozenFrom` on `SplitMoment` (core).
- **Piano ruler** (`ui/PianoRuler.tsx`, `editor/piano.ts`): a 20 px strip of semitone keys on the score's left edge (editor layout), placed per frame by style writes; scale tones and the tonic of the key at the selected moment (else the playhead) marked. One chord selected: its notes pressed, a key toggles a note (freezing; `count` follows; 1..pool size, refusals announced). One pin selected: a key moves it. Otherwise a click auditions the pitch (stopped transport only). Roving tabindex, ↑/↓, Enter.
- **Tests:** unit `m5-helpers.test.ts` (selection, clip/parse/paste/anchor/schema refusal, Freeze keeps the sound and stops following earlier edits, piano helpers, toggled chord compiles), `m5-actions.test.ts` (piano edits, freeze, cut/paste, snap, shortcut map); `canvas-input.test.ts` mock extended. e2e `multi-select`, `copy-paste` (also firefox, except the synthetic-paste test), `piano-ruler`, `freeze` (shared `m5-fixture.ts`); reran lane, lane-dense, lane-rows, lane-preview, score-select, dots, note-points, inspector, render, score-hover, editor-polish, mouse-only, octave-drag, space-key, snap, walk-steps, piece-key (chromium-dev): green.
## M5 touch editing for tablets (app, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §11.3b, §15 M5. Pointer Events only; mouse paths unchanged (a mouse never long-presses and keeps its slop).
- **Audit:** lane, dots, canvas, ruler, divider and inspector scrubs were already Pointer Events with `touch-action: none`, so tap/drag basically worked. Missing: finger-sized thresholds, any Alt/Shift substitute, long press, tap readout (hover only), two-finger pan, vertical lane scroll by finger (the track takes every touch), and the palette's hover preview fired on touch. iPad trap: Safari also sends `gesturechange` for a touch pinch, so the score zoomed twice; ignored while two pointers are down.
- **`app/touch.ts`:** `slopFor` (finger ≥ 10 px; canvas 4, lane and dots 3 for mouse/pen), `startLongPress` (500 ms, cancelled past 10 px; finger or pen), double tap within 24 px (`isDoublePress` takes a px), latched modifiers `useTouchMods` and the menu state.
- **Long press** (lane item, piece key, lane background, a score line → its moment, score background) opens `ui/TouchMenu.tsx`: Duplicate (copies land right after the group's span end, at least a beat or 1 s later; seeds kept; selected), Copy, Delete, Paste at playhead (when something was copied; `clipboard.ts` gained `copySelection`/`pasteHere`/`hasClip`), and three latched toggles: **Snap** (off = Alt: free time, chromatic pitch; lane, dots, note points), **Ends set stagger** (= Shift on a span-end drag; only on glide moments), **Select more** (= Shift/⌘: taps toggle selection on lane, lines and dots; a lane background drag draws the marquee, adding). Latched modifiers show as chips bottom-centre; tapping a chip turns it off. Items react only to a press that began on the menu (the opening finger's release can't pick one). Product choices (integrator may revisit): latched rather than one-shot; Copy/Paste and Select more added beyond the spec's list, since a tablet has no keys.
- **No hover:** a tap on a line shows the voice/note/Hz readout (kept until the next press); the palette's hover preview is mouse-only (tap adds at the playhead). Greyed palette buttons still explain themselves only in the tooltip.
- **Gestures:** two fingers pan the score as well as pinch-zoom; a finger drag on the lane background pans time and scrolls the rows. Fingers (`pointer: coarse`): dots get a 44 px hit area, span-end handles 24 px wide; lane rows keep their height. Controls get `touch-action: manipulation`; no iOS callout on the lane, score, dots and palette.
- **Tests:** unit `touch.test.ts`; e2e `touch-edit.spec.ts` (touch-only context 1180×820, `hasTouch`): palette tap add, tap select, inspector chip by tap, finger drag move + wobble stays a tap, span end length and latched stagger, long-press Duplicate/Delete/outside tap, Snap off, Select more + touch marquee, chord-dot drag, tap readout, score long press and double-tap note, pinch and two-finger pan. Helper `tests/e2e/touch.ts`: CDP touches on Chromium, synthetic touch `PointerEvent`s on Firefox/WebKit (pointer capture on synthetic ids made a no-op by an init script). chromium-dev and firefox-dev green; WebKit not run locally (CI). Reran lane, dots, palette, multi-select, copy-paste, note-points, score-select, octave-drag, mouse-only, lane-dense, snap, snap-beats, score-hover, walk-steps, tune-dots, lane-preview, editor-polish, app, piano-ruler, freeze (chromium-dev) and the cross-engine ones on firefox-dev: green.
- **Not verified:** a real iPad. Not done: pinch on the lane (only the score), touch alternative for Shift-drag octave moves of dots (no menu entry; the inspector's ▲/▼ remain).

## M5 touch gaps closed (app, 2026-11-21)

- **Lane pinch** (`ui/Lane.tsx`): the second finger on the track cancels the first finger's press (drag, pan, marquee, hold) and starts a pinch: spread zooms the window, moving together pans, same maths as the score. The gesture does nothing more until every finger is up.
- **Dot octave by touch**: a long press on a dot (`ArrivalDots.tsx`) selects it (adds with Select more) and opens the touch menu; with dots selected the menu offers **Octave up / Octave down** (`TouchMenu.tsx`, `octaveDots`, one undo entry), also from a long press on empty score.
- **Piano ruler bug (mouse too):** the global `button { min-height: 34px }` made each key 34 px tall, so keys overlapped and a tap or click on a key could land on a lower neighbour. `.piano-key { min-height: 0 }`; `piano-ruler.spec` now checks that keys do not overlap.
- **Tests:** `touch-edit.spec` gained lane pinch and pan, "a pinch that starts on a moment edits nothing", dot long-press octave up/down, and the piano ruler by touch. chromium-dev and firefox-dev green, with `piano-ruler`, `multi-select` and `dots`. `docs/m5-acceptance.md` gaps 3 to 5 removed.
## M6-a timbre DSP depth: FM, noise, stretch (dsp + core, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §8.4, §8.5, §15 M6. Engine only (no UI).
- **Routing:** `TimbreSlot` builds a `TimbreExt` per installed set (null for timbres with no FM, noise or stretch). `Voice.synth` binds each table role to its ext (by table identity, on change only) and sends a tick to `synthExt` only if an active role has one; plain timbres keep the old loops untouched. `render()` is unchanged. Render goldens (`tests/dsp/render-golden.{test.ts,json}`, new: 15 fixtures + 8 example pieces, 20 s each) pinned on c31ed0a and unchanged; `preset-bell` added.
- **FM:** carrier read at φ + β/2π·sin(2π·φm); φm is a per-role accumulator advancing by r·inc (any real r ≤ 32). `index` = β at middle C (radians), × 2^(−indexTrack·oct). Cap: H·f + (H·β + 3)·r·f ≤ 0.9·Nyquist (H = top nonzero partial multiple); harmonic n of a table carrier (partial i: R_i) sees n·β. β interpolates per sample across ticks.
- **Noise:** per-voice xorshift32 (seeded by voice hash and piece seed) → RBJ band-pass at f (−3 dB width `bandwidth`·f), gain √(1.5(1+α)/α)·level, so RMS = level × the tone's; under the voice envelope. Roles mix by level-weighted bandwidth.
- **Additive (`stretch` ≠ 0, −0.5..1):** the nonzero partials of the first 16 at n^(1+stretch), own phases, shared sine table; per-mip amplitude rows (tilt per octave of the ratio, track, formants, unit-sine RMS at C4) interpolated by the role's mip weight; fade 0.85–0.95·Nyquist, skipped above. After a gap (silence, seek) phases restart from the carrier phase (ψ_i = R_i·φc + c_i), so fresh voices follow their group's phase. **Trap:** for inharmonic partials the unison lock's group gain h is computed from φc only, so a locked bell unison is only approximately level-matched; noise is uncorrelated, so a locked noise-only unison sits 3 dB under one voice.
- **Schema:** stretch −0.5..1; fm ratio (0, 32], index 0..20, indexTrack −4..4; noise level 0..4, bandwidth (0, 8] (relative to f; the schema fixture's 300 became 0.3). `docs/file-format.md` regenerated.
- **Bell preset:** 7 partials, stretch 0.12, FM r 1.41 β 0.35 track 0.5, noise 0.04/bw 2, crisp strike 0.75 decaying over 3 s.
- **Numbers (tests/dsp/m6.test.ts):** sidebands k = −3..3 match J_k(1.5)/J_0 within 0.01 dB; click detector on r = 1.37, β = 3: engine 0.212 vs ideal FM 0.212 vs wrapped-phase modulator 1.117 (order 2: 2.78 / 2.80 / 11.6); stretch 0.1 partials within 2e-5 of n^1.1, amplitudes within 0.1 dB; noise RMS within 0.15 dB of level × tone over bw 0.25–2 and 220–2093 Hz, 46 % of power inside the −3 dB band, voices uncorrelated (0.007); stray (aliased) power: FM β 5 at C7/E7/A♯7 −53/−55/−61 dB (uncapped −17 dB), bell at E7/C8 −58/−66 dB, 16-partial stretch 0.3 at C7 −59 dB. Timbre crossfades and table swaps to/from M6 timbres stay under 2× steady; offline = block-by-block. Stems sum ≤ −90 dBFS with M6 timbres. Bell added to the click-detector transport suite.
- **Perf:** M6 workload (24 voices: bell, FM reed, breath; `M6_WORKLOAD_TIMBRES`) RTF 16.2× (plain 21.7×), no-GC 0 scavenges. **Trap:** under Vitest an imported binding read per sample (`SINE`) costs ~3× (module-namespace reads); `synthExt` holds it in a local.
- **Not done:** an FM index envelope (the spec has none; a decaying index would make bells brighter at the strike), UI, preset save/load (M6-c).

## M6-c timbre editor, preset files, four presets (app + core, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §11.3 Sound drawer, §8.5, §15 M6.
- **Sound drawer** (`ui/SoundDrawer.tsx`, `sound.css`; "Sound" toggle beside "Notes", one bottom drawer at a time): pick a timbre, then partials (upright sliders, sqrt curve, +/− 1..32; partials past 16 greyed when stretched), Shape (stretch, brightness tilt/track, detune spread, accent), FM and Noise (on/off switches, then ratio/index/track, level/band), Articulation (amount, attack, decay, Strike Auto/Crisp/Soft = `crisp` absent/true/false), Vibrato (rate, depth, delay, jitter). Formants and drift aren't editable but are kept. Master FX not done (not in this task).
- **Logic** (`editor/timbre-edit.ts`): pure recipes; slider table `TIMBRE_CONTROLS` (lin/log/sqrt curves, 1000 steps, values rounded to the step or 3 significant digits so pieces stay tidy). A drag is one history gesture with updates coalesced to one per frame (each rebuilds tables in the engine); keyboard steps are one `edit` each.
- **Built-ins (product choice):** editing one stores a deep copy under the same name in `Piece.timbres` (an override, as the type allows), so all notes using it change; the select marks it "(edited)" and **Revert** restores the built-in values. **Duplicate** makes `name 2` to edit separately.
- **Preset files** (`core/timbre-preset.ts`): `{ format: 'algorhythms-timbre', version: 1, name, timbre }`, `*.algorhythms-timbre.json`; `timbre` is a TimbreSchema entry (defaults filled, unknown fields kept); newer version loads with a warning; a piece file is refused by name. Documented in `docs/file-format.md` (regenerated). **Load into:** notes using the edited timbre (default), all notes, one note, or none; a clashing name with a different definition becomes `name 2`; one undo entry.
- **Presets** (`core/timbres.ts`): `epiano` (FM 1:1 β 1.4 track 0.6, crisp 0.8 / 1.5 s), `flute` (5 partials, noise 0.18 bw 0.35, soft bloom, vibrato 9 ct), `marimba` (3 partials, stretch 0.98 → 1 : 3.94 : 8.86, noise 0.05, crisp 0.85 / 0.6 s), `brass` (FM 1:1 β 2 track 0.8, swell 0.25, vibrato 5 ct). Older presets unchanged (render goldens pass).
- **Numbers** (`tests/dsp/m6c-presets.test.ts`, reference phrase of 3 gliding lines, 10 s, dry): a strike never exceeds the held level, so levels are compared with the older presets of the same kind (median held −19.17, struck −27.66 dBFS): epiano −0.22, flute +0.40, marimba −2.00, brass −1.37 dB. Click detector worst: pluck/bell 0.479; epiano 0.309, flute 0.222, marimba 0.266, brass 0.287. Stray power on held p96/102/108 ≤ −56 dB for all (the measurement's floor). epiano, flute, marimba added to the transport click suite (`clicks.test.ts`). WAVs: `renders/m6-c/` (main checkout).
- **Tests:** unit `timbre-edit.test.ts` (built-in untouched, revert, partials, switches, load targets and name clashes, slider ranges inside schema bounds, a drag = one entry coalesced per frame), `timbre-preset.test.ts` (built-ins round-trip text-exactly, marker, bounds); property `timbre-preset.property.test.ts` (random valid timbres round-trip exactly; out-of-range refused); e2e `timbre-editor.spec.ts` (drag + undo, keyboard step, FM on, save → file equals the piece's timbre, load into all notes exactly, undo, piece file refused; shot `timbre-editor.png`). `m6.test.ts` "only bell uses an M6 path" now lists the new presets.
- **Trap:** `timbreOptions` (Voices drawer) lists the new presets automatically; M6-b's palette/inspector may want the same list.
## Composition rewrite with rev 9 (examples, 2026-11-21)
docs/composing.md §1. No src change.
- **Generators:** `scripts/compositions/` — `song.ts` (beat grid, chords with `land`, tunes from `pitch:beats` strings, pulses), one file per song, `build.mjs` writes and prettier-formats `examples/compositions/` (now pretty-printed; dropped from `.prettierignore`). `compare.mjs <old-dir> <out-dir> [song|all] [variant]` renders before/after and prints the numbers below.
- **Mapping as briefed:** parts Melody/Chords/Bass/Sparkle; one tune per phrase (melody 0.04 s early, bass 0.12/0.1/0.05 s, as the old pins); chords keep their pitches and land on the downbeat; gains, mix, ranges, seeds kept; the walks and Kite's End chord keep their old ids (same steps, same jitter).
- **Deviations:**
  - Lead-ins (0.5/0.4/0.3 s) removed: the bar grid has no offset, so Snap to beats needs bar 1 at 0. Everything is that much earlier.
  - Night Ferry's arpeggio is five `together` pulses, one per voice, with rests: the old order 0 2 3 4 3 1 per 6-step bar exactly (`updown` cycles 8 steps).
  - Decays fitted to the old level gates by render (pad dry envelope, per-step peaks and troughs): Harbour 0.86, Kite 1.2, Ferry 1.1 s. The briefed ≈ 0.3 s for Ferry is 4 dB quiet in every section. `strike: false` (the old gates had no strikes).
  - Accents are capped at 1, so the old scales (1.4/1.65, 1.1/1.3, Ferry 0.7–1.15) are Chords level moments. A pulse ends at gain 1, so where the old gate's last value carried on (Harbour coda, Kite outro, Ferry end) a level step sets it.
  - Kept quirks of the Python that shaped what the owner heard: Kite's G/B bass is G (GB and G_ are one tuple in kite.py); breathes return to the level current when written; Ferry's bar 63–65 arpeggio fade never applied.
  - Moment ids `hs0001`, `nf0001`, `kd0001`… in time order (6 base62 chars): random ids cost ~500 share-link chars.
  - Tune glides are one length per tune, so leaps no longer get ×1.6 portamento.
- **Size:** moments 483/1435/349 → 92/106/64 (Harbour/Ferry/Kite). Share links (site URL, JSON as stored) 3146/3364/2642 chars; with M5-style default stripping (estimate in `compare.ts`) 2838/3051/2338. **The ≤ 2,000 target is not met**: the shell alone (meta, mix, voices) is ~900 chars and the notes, voicings and times are the rest; dropping breathes, merging tunes and moving the chord glide into `defaults` still leaves ~2,100–2,500. Needs an M5 decision (more compact ids/encoding, or a higher limit for the compositions).
- **Comparison (new vs old, old shifted by its lead-in):**
  - Pitch every 50 ms, |Δ| > 0.5 st: melody 0.6/0.7/0.3 %, longest run ≤ 0.15 s (inside glides). Bass 2.5/0.4/8.8 %, longest ≤ 0.15 s except Harbour's coda leap (0.55 s: 0.4 s glide vs 0.64 s). Chords land 0.65/0.22/0.55 s earlier by design; against old moved that much: 2.7/0.6/1.4 %, the rest Harbour's coda (1.4 s glide now ends on the beat) and Kite's End chord. Sparkle walks identical (Kite's diverges after its fade to 0, at the walk's last tick).
  - Loudness > 150 Hz: Δ +0.15/+0.01/−0.01 dB. 4 s windows: 2/1/0 off by > 1 dB (Harbour +1.4/−1.1 at 36–44 s: every part alone is within 0.3 dB there, so it is phase between parts; Ferry +1.1 in 0–4 s: arpeggio voices wait for their first hit at 0.08, not 0.05).
  - Pulse sections, pad dry: level Δ ≤ 0.23 dB (Ferry intro +1.2, as above); per-step peaks Δ −0.3…+0.2 ± 0.5–1.0 dB, accent correlation 0.88–0.95 (Ferry intro 0.80); troughs ±1.3–2.2 dB (chord arrivals moved by land).
  - Clicks (`clickScore` worst, full mix): 0.46→0.53, 0.62→0.62, 0.46→0.47; all < 1, i.e. high-frequency content, no clicks.
- **Pluck:** `nightFerry({ arp: 'pluck' })` (strikes on): pad troughs −8 dB, peaks +1 dB, song −0.15 dB. Rendered for the owner; glass is committed.
- **Tests:** `compositions.test.ts` adds parts, moment limits and a tempo. `migration-v2.test.ts`: the compositions on disk are no longer pinned to their v1 sound; the v1 Kite Day fixture still migrates and hashes `c89cab9187e3dffb`. `lane-dense.spec.ts` needs a dense piece: it opens the v1 Kite Day fixture as a share link (reloads reopen the link). No golden hashed the compositions otherwise.
- **Renders** (owner, AAC): `renders/rev9-compositions/{harbour-song,night-ferry,kite-day}-{before,after}.m4a`, `night-ferry-after-arp-pluck.m4a`.

## Portable DSP math: render goldens on Linux (dsp, 2026-11-21)
`render-golden.test.ts` passed on the Mac (arm64) and failed on Linux x64 and CI: `fixture:merge`, Kite Day, Night Ferry, Seven Doors, Cartographers.
- **Cause: `Math.exp`.** V8's `Math.exp` is fdlibm C (`base/ieee754.cc`); clang on arm64 contracts its polynomial into FMAs, x64 does not. On Linux, replacing `Math.exp` by a JS port of fdlibm exp with FMA (emulated) at the Horner steps and `x − t·P` reproduced 22 of the 23 Mac hashes, incl. all five failures (without FMA the port equals Linux `Math.exp` on every argument). The FMA variant differs on ~0.05 % of the arguments the renders use (`voice.ts` `Math.exp(oct·LN2)` phase increment, loudness, articulation; `graph.ts`); unison-lock feedback turns some of those ulps into different samples. 1-ulp nudges of every result of `sin`, `cos`, `tan`, `pow`, `log`, `atan2` changed no hash; `log2` (like `exp`) changes all, so it carries the same risk (Seven Doors, the 23rd, has `log2`/`pow` arguments no other render uses).
- **Fix:** `src/dsp/pmath.ts` (dsp-internal, no core import): `exp2`, `exp`, `log2`, `ln`, `pow`, `dbToGain`, `sinPi`, `cosPi`, `sin`, `cos`, `tan`, `atan2`, built from + − × ÷, floor/round/abs and DataView bits. Algorithms mirror core (`ease` exp2/sinPi, `math` log2): same bits. Every `Math.exp/pow/log/log2/sin/cos/tan/atan2` in `src/dsp` replaced (voice, graph, wavetable, sine table, tone, reverb, limiter, mix). `core/` unchanged.
- **Allocation-free `process()`:** V8 boxes doubles across calls it does not inline, and native Math is a builtin, so plain calls in hot paths allocated (no-GC: 111 scavenges per 60 s). Register forms (`R[0..1]` in, `exp2R()`, `log2R()`, `powR()`, `sinCosR()`, `atan2R()`, … out in `R[0]`) make no JS calls, so they never box; the plain forms wrap them. Two spots inline the bodies with pmath's exported constants: `Voice.render` (3× exp2: inlining even the register calls pushed `segmentPitch`/`segmentMotion` out of render's inlining budget) and `computeGroups`' snap angles (atan2: rarely reached, ran unoptimised and boxed). Rare paths (FX smoothing on mix changes, birth probes) call register forms and may box a few bytes until optimised.
- **Lint (deviation from PLAN §7.11 "the DSP is exempt"):** `src/dsp` now has core's ban on non-portable Math members and non-integer `**`, so a native call cannot sneak back into the render. `lint-rules.test.ts` asserts it. The integrator may revert this block in `eslint.config.js` if PLAN keeps the exemption; the goldens would then catch regressions only on the platform that pinned them. `render-golden.test.ts` also stubs those Math members to throw while it renders.
- **Sound:** offline renders of all 23 goldens, before vs after: worst peak difference −144.5 dBFS (long-glide), typically −150…−168; RMS difference ≤ −195 dBFS. pmath vs Math: relative 1e-13 (exp, pow), 1e-15 (log2), absolute ≤ 4e-14 (cos, |x| ≤ 10), 1e-15 (atan2) (`tests/dsp/pmath.test.ts`, with pinned output bits).
- **Goldens:** all 23 in `render-golden.json` re-pinned on Linux x64 (every render moves by a few float32 ulps). **They must be checked on macOS** (`pnpm vitest run tests/dsp/render-golden.test.ts` on the Mac): CI's macOS job runs WebKit e2e only, not unit tests. `m6.test.ts` compares additive partial ratios to `Math.pow` within 1e-13 instead of exactly.
## M6-b1: the `timbre` moment (core, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §6.3, §7.9 timbre timeline, §15 M6. Core only.
- **Moment:** `TimbreMoment { type: 'timbre', preset, fade }` plus the common fields (`glide` and `overrides` ignored; never lands). Schema: `preset` a non-empty string, `fade` ≥ 0, default 2 s (`MOMENT_DEFAULTS.timbre`; PLAN gives none). Canonical fade 1 ms. Share links strip `fade: 2` through the schema; the frozen share dictionary has no timbre fragment (links still work, a little longer). `docs/file-format.md` regenerated.
- **Compile:** at `at` the change is recorded on each targeted voice (`VoiceRun.timbre`); `finish` builds `CompiledVoice.timbre` from the fresh `VoiceDef.timbre` and those records. No touch or reaim (as a pulse tick), so pitch, lives, levels, weights, harmony and keys are untouched; span `at..at + fade`; like every moment it extends the last activity (to `at + fade`), so a late one moves an open end.
- **Deviation (as briefed): silent voices change too.** `'all'`, `{ voices }` and `{ part }` pick pool voices whether sounding or not (§6.1 selectors pick active voices), so a later birth sounds the new timbre; `{ rank }` picks sounding voices. A `{ voices }` target changes only the listed pool voices, not the voices merged into that line (a later fork onto another pool voice keeps its own timbre).
- **Timeline rules:** a change to the preset in effect adds no entry (`no-effect` info when it changes no voice); of two at one time the later wins (the t = 0 initial entry is never replaced), so no two entries share a time after the first, and consecutive entries differ.
- **Validation:** missing or empty preset → `invalid-moment` (error); a name that is neither a built-in nor an own key of `piece.timbres` → new `unknown-timbre` (warn), skipped; fade clamped ≥ 0 (NaN, ±∞ → 0). Unknown voice/part warn as usual; no targets → `no-targets`.
- **Incremental:** `VoiceRun.timbre` length is checkpointed (NUMS 22). A different set of non-built-in timbre names in `piece.timbres` forces a full compile (it decides validity); voice timbres and timbre definitions still resume (the `no-effect` check runs in `finish`).
- **Flat:** unchanged layout; `timbreNames` collects every preset on every timeline, so moment presets get an index (TIMBRE_PRESET) and tables.
- **Palette:** `momentEffectAt` handles `timbre` (effect if a targeted voice's timbre at `at` differs); not in `EFFECT_MOMENT_TYPES`.
- **App, not done (out of scope, edits refused):** `pnpm typecheck` fails at three app sites that need a `timbre` entry: `editor/actions.ts` LABELS, `ui/Inspector.tsx` TYPE_NAMES, `editor/new-moment.ts` `defaultMoment` switch.
- **Tests:** unit `timbre-moment.test.ts` (targets, silent voices, births, unknown preset/voice/part, fade clamp, no-op and same-time rules, land, end, trace, no pitch change on every example and with parts/merges/splits, flat round trip, incremental = full through timbre, voice-timbre and piece-timbre edits, schema round trip and stripping, palette effect); property: timbre moments in `compiler-arbitraries` (incl. `mine` and an unknown preset), "muting timbre moments changes only the timelines", incremental edits of voice timbres and of a piece timbre `mine`. Two tests that used `timbre` as the unknown type now use `camera`.
- **Numbers:** compile benchmark median 37–41 ms on this cloud box vs 38.6 ms for 5020450 on the same box (no change; the Mac measured ~8 ms). `render-golden` fails with the same 5 hashes as 5020450.

## M6-b3: the `timbre` moment in the editor (app, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §11.2 Effects row, §11.3 palette/inspector, §15 M6. App only; on `m6-b1`.
- **Typecheck fixed:** `actions.ts` LABELS `timbre change`, Inspector TYPE_NAMES `Timbre`, `defaultMoment` case `timbre`.
- **Default** (`new-moment.ts` `newTimbrePreset`): the first of `timbreOptions(piece)` (piece's own, then built-ins) that no pool voice plays at `at` (compiled timbre timelines, silent voices included, as the default target 'all' picks them); if all are in use, the first that differs from the most common. Fade `MOMENT_DEFAULTS.timbre.fade` (2 s). Tidewater Hymn (all glass) gets `sine`.
- **Palette:** `timbre`, icon ◐, key **B** (the only free letter of "timbre"), last. `'timbre'` in `EXTRA_TYPES`. 14 buttons wrapped at 1280 px (palette 944 px wide on this box), so palette buttons are narrower again: padding 6px 3px, icon gap 3 px (~23 px to spare here; checked by `timbre-moment.spec`). Inspector hint lists B.
- **Inspector** (`TypeFields.tsx` `TimbreFields`): Timbre menu (`timbreOptions`, marked "(edited)", "(piece)", "(missing)" for an unknown preset), Fade (0–120 s, slider 0–10). Part and Advanced targeting as for level; no transition or seed (`pitched` excludes timbre).
- **Lane:** new `LaneRow` `'effects'` under Level, 16 px sub-rows stacked like Moments (marker, label, span); hidden while `count.effects` is 0, so it appears with the first timbre moment or a timbre ghost (hover, palette drag). Label = moment label, else the preset. Bar `at..at+fade` (compiled span, else `estimateSpan`), gradient, no length text. End drag sets `fade` (`EndEdit 'fade'`, undo "Change fade", announced).
- **Sound drawer (new controls; the drawer had no rename or delete):** for the piece's own timbres (`isCustom`), **Rename** (inline field: Enter commits, a taken/empty/built-in name is refused with the reason, Escape cancels) renames the `timbres` key in place, its notes and every timbre moment's `preset` as one undo entry; **Delete** only when no note uses it (disabled otherwise), leaving its moments as `unknown-timbre`. The users line also counts timbre changes. Recipes in `timbre-edit.ts` (`renameTimbre`, `timbreRenameError`, `deleteTimbre`, `timbreMoments`).
- **Tests:** unit `tests/unit/app/timbre-moment-ui.test.ts` (default preset incl. silent voices, later changes and the all-in-use fallback; palette key and effect; Effects row items, stacking, fade drag; rename/refusals/delete + diagnostic; rename = one undo entry); `shortcuts`/`lane` tests updated for 14 entries and the `effects` count. E2E `timbre-moment.spec.ts` (palette one row at 1280 px; add from the palette, preset and fade in the inspector, Effects bar width, end drag, undo back to no row; Sound drawer rename/delete). `palette.spec` (14 buttons, B), `lane-preview.spec` (timbre ghost on Effects).
- **Results:** lint, typecheck clean; `pnpm test` all pass except `render-golden` (the known 5 hashes). E2E chromium-dev: timbre-moment, palette, lane-preview, lane-rows, lane-dense, timbre-editor, pulse-ui, tune-ui, inspector, mouse-only, context-moments (55 passed); timbre-moment on chromium-prod too. Firefox not installed here. Browsers via `PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH` symlinks (1255 → 1243), as before.

## Fix: knot in the ghost where lines split (render, 2026-11-21)
Owner: "artifacts in front of the playhead where lines split".
- **Cause:** the future is drawn per line at `futureOpacity` (0.35). At a merge or split the strands overlap by construction (tiling + `STRAND_LAP`, offsets that shrink as they peel, then near-coincident glides), so their alpha stacked: up to 1 − 0.65ⁿ, a dark knot on light paper and a bright one on dark. The past is opaque, so it never showed there.
- **Fix** (`render/ghost.ts`, new): per bundle ahead of the playhead, a box (device px) from the bundle time out over its span until every strand is ≥ 1 px clear of its neighbours (scan every 3 px, cached per bundle and view scale); `Bundle.lo/hi` (strands.ts) bound the scan: the crossfade window widened to the strands' arriving/departing glides, ≤ 8 s. The bundle's strands are drawn at full strength into an offscreen canvas inside the boxes (one rect clip each) and composited once at the ghost opacity (× the selection dim); outside the boxes they are drawn directly under one even-odd clip. Other lines, and frames with no bundle ahead, take the old path. The selected future (0.6) goes through the layer the same way. No layer where none can be made (Node tests): direct drawing as before.
- **Not changed:** the past (pixel-identical before/after in headless chromium), frames with no bundle ahead (identical), opaque ghost (`futureOpacity` 1, before the first play). Member lines outside the boxes are drawn under the clip, so their AA edges can differ by ≤ 9/255. Lines at partial level opacity still stack where they overlap, in the past as in the future (separate issue).
- **Rejected:** one layer for the whole future (simple, exact, but +4–8 ms per frame in software raster: the composite of the full future region); blending toward the paper colour (needs CSS colour parsing; oklch stops are out of sRGB gamut, so the browser's mapping can't be matched).
- **Perf** (`render.spec` @perf, chromium-dev, this Linux box, software raster, 2 runs each, before → after): ink-24 flush 4.7–5.1 → 4.5–4.8; pitch-24 flush 6.9–7.2 → 6.6–7.1; pitch-24 draw-only 2.1–2.2 → 2.8–3.7; ink-24 draw-only 2.1–2.3 → 2.8; overview 7.0–7.2 → 8.1–8.5. Draw-only rises because `drawImage` rasterises the layer inside `drawScore`. The strict 4 ms gate fails on this box before and after.
- **Tests:** `render.spec` "ghost lines do not stack…" (8 lines merge at 4 s, split at 8 s, all ahead; ink colours; darkest pixel at the merge and split ≤ a lone ghost line + 3/255; before: 122 vs 76, after 76 vs 76). `strands.test.ts`: `Bundle.lo/hi`; with a stubbed `OffscreenCanvas`, one composite per bundle at 0.35, boxes < 5 % of the canvas, strands at full strength in the layer, 0.35 outside; selection dims; past/opaque/no-bundle frames draw the same fills as without a layer. `path-ctx.ts` records `drawImage` and `clip`. Dev entry: `frame()`.
- **Seen, not fixed:** thin AA seams across the rope where strand pieces meet at staggered split events (Tidewater 91.9 s); in the past too.
