## M6-b2: timbre moments in sound (dsp, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §6.2 `timbre`, §8.5, §8.11, §15 M6. On 70b9419 (M6-b1 + M6-b3 + portable math).
- **Mix (`voice.ts` `timbreMix`):** entry j of a voice's timeline fades in over its fade on top of everything before it: weight x_j·Π_{k>j}(1 − x_k), x_j its linear progress. A change arriving mid-fade starts from the mix sounding then (the earlier fade carries on underneath), and the mix is a pure function of time, so seeks and births mid-fade start on it. No stored state; up to 4 distinct timbres at once (a 5th folds into the oldest kept). Changes after t = 0 fade over ≥ 5 ms (`MIN_TIMBRE_FADE`; fade 0 would click). Parameters (vibrato, articulation, drift, detune, accent) are the weighted mean (`Voice.tp`); one timbre copies them bit for bit. No re-strike: strikes still come only from core.
- **Missing tables:** a slot without tables passes its weight to the entry before it (and a replacement fade with no old tables likewise), so a timbre whose tables have not arrived keeps the previous one sounding; they fade in over the usual 20 ms when they arrive. Hosts already build every `timbreNames` table first (offline: `createLoadedGraph`; live: `engine.load`/`swap` post tables before the score), so this is a fallback.
- **Gain law (`balanceRoles`):** at one pitch two timbres share the carrier phase, so they are partly correlated: constant amplitude dips (−4.4 dB sine→epiano at A3), constant power would bump for near-identical spectra. New `WavetableSet.coherence` (built with the tables, 8 rows per octave × 66: phase-locked harmonics 1–32 as complex amplitudes, noise level, power of everything else) gives each table's spectrum as the voice synthesises it: FM via Bessel J_k (Miller recurrence, `besselJ`) with the tracked, capped β; folded negative sidebands; inharmonic partials and off-harmonic sidebands as power only; one noise source per voice, so coherent. The role weights are scaled by one c so the mix's power moves linearly between the timbres' (c = 1 for identical spectra; clamp ×0.5…×2). Sets without coherence keep c = 1.
- **Unison lock:** h assumed every member's waveform is the same. When members sound different timbres (and some member has timbre changes), `Graph.timbreZ2` sums |Z|² pairwise with each pair's correlation from the members' spectra at their last tick (`Voice.publishTimbre`, computed only for such groups). **Gated to groups with a timbre-changing voice**, so static multi-timbre unisons keep their sound bit for bit (goldens); ungated it moves `dense-24` +0.02 dB and `stress` +0.06 dB (≤ 0.13 dB per second). The integrator may drop the gate (the `changesTimbre` check in `timbreZ2`) and re-pin those two.
- **Hot swap:** `swapNeedsCrossfade` compares the timbre mixes (any slot's weight moving > 0.01 crossfades) instead of the entry in force.
- **Numbers (`tests/dsp/timbre-moment.test.ts`):** level during a 2 s fade vs power moving linearly between the ends, worst of A3/C4/C5 (constant amplitude in brackets): glass→sine 0.03 (−0.06), glass→bell −0.22 (−0.75), sine→epiano 0.05 (−4.40), choir→bell 0.38 (−2.26), sine→reed −0.02 (−0.51) dB (articulation, vibrato, drift off). Coherence power predicts every preset's level against the sine within 0.09 dB. Locked pair, one voice changing: level vs the chord sum moves ≤ 0.08 dB (before: −1.33 sine→epiano, −0.47 glass→bell, −1.9 choir→bell). Real glass→bell→glass at C4: −19.2 → −27.4 dB (bell's held level is 0.25) with no window outside the ends. Seek mid-fade: weights equal within 1e-12, level 0.003 dB off; a voice born mid-fade renders the same from 0 or from a seek before its birth (< 1e-6). Clicks (worst at changes / clean): path switches with five timbres overlapping and a fade-0 change, A2/E4/A5: 0.178/0.159, 0.304/0.372, 0.653/0.629; glass→bell→glass 0.232/0.336; transport suite `timbre changes` (seeks and loop wraps mid-fade): clean 0.371, seek/pause 0.325, loop 0.282, hard cut 1.63.
- **No-GC:** new `timbre` run (lock workload with seeks, every voice changing every ~1.5 s over 0.4–2.4 s fades through every path, `withTimbreChanges`): 0 GC lines, 1864 B (as the other runs). **Traps:** (1) an imported *or exported* module constant in a phi with a double boxes it (the FM limits moved to wavetable.ts made `synthExt` allocate 1 MB/s; voice.ts keeps its own copies, `fmLimits()` lets a test compare); (2) the timbre part of `probe` inlined into it pushed `segmentPitch` out of probe's inlining budget (~850 B per seek): it is a separate `probeMix`, called by the graph after `probe`.
- **Goldens:** the 24 existing hashes unchanged; new fixture `timbre-moments` (5 voices, overlapping fades across every path, a locked pair changing apart) pinned on Linux x64. Stems sum ≤ −90 dBFS with it; offline = block by block.
- **Perf:** RTF on this box, three alternating runs vs 70b9419: plain 11.4/11.2/11.4× (base 11.3/11.4/10.9×), M6 6.8× (base 6.6–6.8×; the M6 gate fails at base here too). A first version cost 18 % (9.3×): the per-block spectrum publish and copying 13 parameters every tick; now `publishTimbre` runs on demand and `tp` is copied only when the slot or its tables change (`TimbreSlot.version`).
- **Renders (owner, offline, 24-bit):** `renders/m6-b/tidewater-hymn-original.wav`, `…-glass-bell-glass.wav` (bell 20 s/4 s, glass 47 s/4 s, epiano 68 s/6 s retargeted to sine at 71 s/4 s, glass 92 s/3 s), `…-half-bell-unison.wav` (odd voices to bell at 76 s before the 82 s unison, back at 104 s). 4 s RMS: the bell stretch sits ~4–8 dB under glass (its held level), the sine stretch ~3 dB over (no articulation).
- **Playhead labels:** overlapping equal pitches show only `N Hz` (no ×count); one label per pitch kept.

- **Next hold (2026-11-21, owner):** `suggestionTime` = max(latest unmuted end, latest unmuted `at`) + a hold (one bar with Snap to beats, then the next beat; else 2 s); 0 with no moments. A fresh piece's Next is at 0:02, not stacked on the opening chord. Compose doesn't use it (unchanged).

## Tune: Melody on (core + app, 2026-11-21)

Owner: "the tune moment should let me pick what note has the melody."
- **Core:** `TuneMoment.line?: 'low' | 'high'` (`TuneLine`): the target's lowest or highest line in pitch order at `at` carries the tune; absent = 'low', so existing pieces, goldens and links are unchanged. Schema `optional()` (no default, so `stripDefaults` keeps a written value; the app never writes 'low'); `docs/file-format.md` regenerated. Compiler `onTune` (first note, and re-picking after the line is lost) and the palette check (`effect.ts`) both honour it. No flat-layout or share-dict change.
- **Inspector** (`TuneFields.tsx` `MelodyOn`, first row of the tune's fields): "Melody on" lists the sounding notes high to low (key spelling, "· <part>" when the piece has parts) between "Highest of <part>" and "Lowest of <part>" ("Highest/Lowest note" for the all target, "of the target" for rank/multi-voice). Pitches are where each line is heading at `at`, the tune's own line before its first move (`melodyOptions`, `editor/tune.ts`). A note writes `{ voices: [id] }` and drops `line`; Highest/Lowest keep the target (a picked voice goes back to its part, or all without parts) and set or drop `line` (`melodyPatch`). One undo entry ("Change melody line"); auditions the first note on the new line (`auditionTuneAfterEdit`, stopped transport only). Existing tunes show "Lowest of <part>". A picked voice that isn't sounding is listed "(not sounding)". Advanced targeting unchanged (a picked note shows as Pick… there and "Advanced" in Part).
- **Score:** the selected tune's line highlight follows the compile, so it moves with the pick. Click-to-move on another line **not done**: a click on a line already selects the moment owning that section (`canvas-input.ts clickAt`), which conflicts.
- **Palette default (product call, for the owner to confirm):** a new tune keeps the 'all' target but with more than one line sounding sets `line: 'high'` and starts its notes from the top line's pitch (melody on top). One line: unchanged.
- **Tests:** `unit/core/tune.test.ts` (low/high on a 3-line part, voices target, absent = 'low', incremental edits of `line`/target, schema round-trip and bad value); `unit/app/tune-melody.test.ts` (menu, labels, current choice, patches, palette default); `e2e/tune-ui.spec.ts` "Melody on" (pick a note, the compiled line moves, highest, undo ×2).
- **Status:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=3` 1833/1833 (goldens unchanged). E2E chromium-dev and -prod `--workers=1`: tune-ui, tune-dots, inspector, palette 62/62.

## Bell redesign (dsp + core, 2026-11-21)
Owner on `renders/m6-b/tidewater-hymn-glass-bell-glass.m4a`: "the bell sounds awful". On d3c26be.
- **Diagnosis** (4-voice C-major chord held, dry, 2.5–3.9 s after the strike; single C4): the M6-a bell holds its whole spectrum. 34 % of held power is off the harmonic series (stretched partials at 2.17/3.42/4.73× = octave +1.3 st, twelfth +2.3 st; FM sidebands at 0.41/0.76/2.40/3.58×). Strongest beats 14–21 Hz at −12 dB (the roughest beat range). Sethares roughness 3.3 against glass's 0.41; without FM it is 0.42, so FM makes the roughness and stretch the mistuning. The spectrum doesn't change over time (each partial sits at the same level relative to the fundamental at 0.05 s and at 5 s): the strike only makes the whole tone 16 dB louder, and it falls to a held level of 0.25. Held, the bell is 8 dB under glass; −3.9 dB on the chord and −5.8 dB on Tidewater 0–40 s. Its noise (0.04) also sustains as hiss.
- **DSP, two optional fields** (absent = bit-identical; render goldens unchanged except `fixture:preset-bell` and `fixture:timbre-moments`, the only ones using bell):
  - `Timbre.strikePartials` (≤ 16 of `{ratio, amp, decay}`: ratio (0, 32], amp ≤ 4 relative to the tone, decay to 1 % in (0, 60] s). Sines that sound only after a strike, each under the articulation envelope's shape (same attack, the same rise from the previous strike's value) with its own decay. Shaped per mip by `brightness.track`, faded near Nyquist like partials, no FM, not in `coherence`. `render` publishes the envelope as A + B·2^(−k·X/D) terms (`S_ENV_*`, only when the mix's `TimbreParams.strikeEnv` > 0). `synthExt` evaluates it per partial and tick with `pmath.exp2R`, and skips a partial below −96 dB.
  - `fm.held` (0..1, default 1): the index is β·(held + (1 − held)·env), env being the articulation envelope with its decay; `coherence` uses the held index.
- **Candidates** (`tests/dsp/bell.test.ts` `BELL_CANDIDATES`):
  - **1-church** (now `bell`): harmonic tone [1, 0.4, 0, 0.1], with a church-bell strike: hum 0.5 (8 s), tierce 1.2 (1.8 s), quint 1.5, upper partials 2.51–8.2 (0.9→0.12 s). Articulation 0.45 over 2.5 s; no noise.
  - **2-fm-strike**: harmonic tone, FM 3.5:1 with β 2.4 at the strike, ringing down to 0 (`held` 0) over 1.8 s.
  - **3-glass-bell**: the glass partials and vibrato held; metal-bar strike partials 2, 2.756, 5.404, 8.933, 13.34 (1.6→0.15 s).

  | | held off-harmonic C / F | roughness held (strike) | strike − held | chord / Tidewater vs glass |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | 0-current | 34.1 / 31.6 % | 3.29 (3.14) | 16.2 dB | −3.9 / −5.8 dB |
  | 1-church | 0.5 / 1.2 % | 0.20 (0.62) | 11.4 dB | −0.2 / −0.8 dB |
  | 2-fm-strike | 0.0 / 0.0 % | 0.16 (1.77) | 9.0 dB | −0.9 / −1.3 dB |
  | 3-glass-bell | 0.6 / 0.8 % | 0.41 (0.40) | 10.2 dB | −0.2 / −0.5 dB |
  | glass | 0.6 / 0.8 % | 0.41 (0.17) | 7.2 dB | 0 |

  Single C4, off-harmonic share at 0.01–0.35 s → at 2.5 s: church 22.5 → 1.0 %, fm 62.7 → 0.0 %, glass-bell 12.1 → 0.0 %, current 30.5 → 31.8 %. The strongest held beats are now 1.8 Hz at −19 dB (detune between coinciding harmonics).
- **Tests:** new `bell.test.ts`: defaults are bit-identical; struck partials match amp·e^(−ln100·τ/decay) within 0.001 dB (the output lags piece time by the 5 ms limiter lookahead); a seek mid-ring lands within 0.05 dB; the top of the range has stray power ≤ −57 dB; `held` 0.5 gives J1/J0 at β/2 exactly; offline = block-by-block; the candidate table above.
  - `M6A_BELL` (fixtures) keeps the old bell where tests need its paths or numbers: the m6 aliasing and params tests, `m6c-presets` (the presets were levelled against it), the every-path timbre switch, `M6_WORKLOAD_TIMBRES.bell`, so the RTF workload costs the same. The new `strikebell` (M6-a plus struck partials and `held` 0.5) is in the no-GC `m6`/`timbre` runs and the stems test.
  - **Deviation:** the `clicks.test.ts` positive control now asks for median > threshold instead of 1.5 × threshold. Two unrelated stretches of RMS σ meet with a median step of about 0.95σ. With the new bell, `timbre changes` has clean 0.331, and its hard cut scores 0.93 against the old bar of 0.99.
  - Property arbitraries cover the new fields.
- **Perf:**
  - no-GC: all runs 0 GC lines, 1864 B.
  - RTF: plain 11.4×; M6 6.7× (6.7–6.9× before; the gate fails at base on this box).
  - Same workload with each bell (vite runner): M6-a 6.3–6.8×, new built-in 6.8–6.9×, `strikebell` 5.6–5.8×.
- **Renders (owner, AAC 192k, Tidewater mix):** `renders/bell/bell-{0-current,1-church,2-fm-strike,3-glass-bell}-{chord,tidewater}.m4a`.
  - chord: the chord above with reverb, a glide and re-strike at 4–5.2 s and 8.5–9.3 s, 13 s.
  - tidewater: Tidewater Hymn 0–40 s, every voice on that bell, 1 s fade at the end.
## Tune: random melody and double-click notes (core + app, 2026-11-21)
Owner: "tune should also make a random melody; while selecting a tune moment, double clicking on that note should produce another note in the tune there."
- **Core** (`core/tune-gen.ts randomTune`, new `Purpose.TuneGen` = 17): seeded, pure. Rhythm on a grid (an eighth with a tempo, else 0.25 s): 4-unit cells ([2,2], [1,1,2], [3,1], [2,1,1], [4], [1,1,1,1]) from a 3-cell palette in a form (AABA, ABAB, AABB, ABAC), ending on a 4-unit note (a pickup before it when the length isn't whole cells); the last note takes any remainder, so lengths sum to the target. Pitch: scale degrees, first note within a 3rd of the line's pitch; repeat 8 %, step 62 %, 3rd 17 %, 4th 9 %, 5th 4 % (no two repeats in a row; a 4th/5th is followed by a step back); arch contour; window ±5 degrees of the start, moved inside the line's range; last note the nearest tone of the harmony at the tune's end (`chordAt(...).pcs` in the key), else tonic/fifth.
- **App** (`editor/tune.ts`): `randomMelody` (key at `at`; line = the compile's first tune segment's `p0`, else the sounding line `line`/single-voice target picks; its range; length Σlen, or `newTuneLength`: two bars of `view.meter` (else 4) beats, or 4 s) and `insertTuneNote`. Inspector "Random melody" (`tune-random`): new `randomU32` seed each press, one undo entry ("Random melody"), auditions the whole melody on its line (`auditionMelodyAfterEdit`, ≤ 8 s, stopped transport only). Palette/E: new tunes get `randomMelody` seeded by `momentSeed(piece.seed, id)` (so the hover ghost matches the drop).
- **Double-click** (`canvas-input.ts addTuneNoteAt`): when the selection *at the first click* was exactly one tune, a double-click/double-tap anywhere on the score inserts into it (pitch `dragPitch`: key snap, Alt chromatic; time to beats with Snap to beats, Alt free), re-selects the tune, one undo entry ("Add tune note"), auditions the note. Otherwise the note-point double-click is unchanged.
- **Product calls:** (1) No key hint: every free letter is a palette key, and a modified letter would be a first. (2) Double-click on a note's start (within 0.05 s; common with beat snap): the new note takes that note's first half. (3) The selection at the first click decides, so a first click that selects another line's moment doesn't cancel the insert, and double-clicking an *unselected* tune's line still adds a note point. (4) Random melody auditions the whole melody, not one note.
- **Examples** (seeds 101…505, C major, G4 line, range G3–C6, 2 bars at 120, harmony C E G; lengths in beats):
  - E4 ½, G4 ½, B4 ½, A4 ½, B4 1, A4 1, C5 ½, G4 ½, A4 ½, B4 ½, C5 2
  - G4 1½, D4 ½, E4 1½, A4 ½, G4 1, F4 1, E4 2
  - A4 2, B4 2, C5 1, G4 ½, A4 ½, G4 2
  - G4 1, C5 ½, B4 ½, C5 1, F4 1, G4 1, F4 ½, D4 ½, C4 2
  - E4 ½, F4 ½, G4 1, B4 2, B4 ½, D5 ½, A4 1, G4 2
- **Tests:** unit `core/tune-gen.test.ts` (determinism, key, range, sum, grid lengths, ≥ 80 % moves ≤ 2 degrees, cadence, repeated cells, short totals; `TUNE_EXAMPLES=1` prints the examples), `app/tune-insert.test.ts` (split, on a start, append, prepend, empty, clamps; randomMelody line/range/length/beats); editor and tune-melody default-tune tests updated. e2e: tune-ui "Random melody" (palette test: the ghost and E now give a 2-bar melody), tune-dots "double-click adds a note" (split, append, prepend, undo) and "no tune selected: note point".
- **Status:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=3` 1847/1847 (goldens unchanged); e2e:quick tune-ui tune-dots note-points touch-edit palette, chromium-dev/prod `--workers=1` 64/64.
## M6 master FX and bell fields in the Sound drawer (app, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §8.6, §11.3, §15 M6. On bd801d6.
- **Master tab** (`ui/SoundDrawer.tsx`, `editor/mix-edit.ts`): the drawer has Timbre | Master tabs; Master edits `Piece.mix`: Reverb (room size, decay, damping, pre-delay, return), Delay (time, feedback, return), Sends (master reverb/delay send, Auto pan by note/pitch), Tone (tilt, low-pass); each section has Reset to `defaultMix()` (disabled at the defaults; Sends also resets `panAuto`). One line notes the limiter (always on, its ceiling). `MIX_CONTROLS` share `SliderControl` with `TIMBRE_CONTROLS` (`valueOf`/`positionOf`/`roundFor`): log for decay, delay time, low-pass; sqrt for levels and pre-delay; ranges inside the schema and the engine's clamps (delay ≤ 2 s, feedback ≤ 0.9, pre-delay ≤ 0.25 s, tilt ±6). Drags reuse the timbre gesture (one undo entry, one update per frame); every new `piece.mix` already reaches the engine via `mixAudio` (store), undo included.
- **Delay in beats:** with a tempo (`gridBpm`) the readout is `dotted 1/8 · 0.75 beats · 0.45 s`; with Snap to beats on, the slider snaps (log-nearest) to 1/16, 1/8, dotted 1/8, 1/4, dotted 1/4, 1/2 that fit ≤ 2 s; Alt is free.
- **Per-note sends** (`ui/VoicesDrawer.tsx`, `editor/voices.ts setVoiceSend/sendOf`): were not in the Voices drawer; new Sends column (R, D sliders, dim while following the master; ↺ clears the note's own). Part headers have no sends control.
- **Bell fields:** `fm.held` slider in FM (absent reads 1: `SliderControl.fallback`); "Strike partials" collapsed `<details>` list of ratio/level/decay number fields (`STRIKE_CONTROLS`: ratio 0.01–32 to 0.001, level 0–4 to 0.001, decay 0.01–60 s to 3 sig. digits), +Add (first 2.76× 0.5 1 s, then an octave above the last at half its level), × per row, max 16; removing the last drops the field.
- **Product calls:** (1) Master is a tab, not more sections under the timbre: with everything in one panel the drawer hit its 46vh cap and the score shrank to ~80 px, where a redraw costs ~1.5 s in headless Chromium on Kite Day (see trap). (2) Delay snap set adds 1/16 and dotted 1/8 to the four asked (the default 0.375 s is a dotted 1/8 at 120). (3) Strike partials are number fields, not sliders (compact; ratios like 2.756 need typing).
- **Click safety** (`tests/dsp/clicks.test.ts`, "mix changes"): `setMix` jumps of every field (reverb decay/damping/pre-delay/return, delay time/feedback/return, sends, pan) and per-frame drags (decay, size, low-pass, delay time at 0.34 s/s) are click-free on sine and glass. **Known gaps (dsp follow-up, `it.fails`):** a jump of tone tilt (glass 2.07 vs clean 0.27; dragged 0.56 vs 0.23, borderline), low-pass 18 k → 600 Hz (0.61 vs 0.08), room size 0.6 → 0.15 (0.36 vs 0.09) clicks: `Tone`/`Reverb` step coefficients once per block (and the tilt shelves skip their state while tilt is 0). A big delay-time jump while the delay is loud swoops (sine 0.28–0.47 vs 0.07): the smoothed read pointer races; a crossfade would fix it. Reset and a fast drag can hit these.
- **Trap (render perf, not fixed):** on Kite Day / Harbour Song with the score squeezed to ~80 px tall, one redraw takes 1.1–1.7 s in headless Chromium (profile: `easeRaw`, `query.subdivide`, `tracePiece`, `outlineFrame`); at 143 px (Notes drawer open) 108 ms, 358 px 69 ms.
- **Tests:** unit `mix-edit.test.ts` (ranges inside `MixSchema` at both ends, log curves, clean numbers, defaults inside sliders, reset per section, delay snap/Alt/no tempo/slow tempo, per-note sends, a drag = one entry coalesced per frame and each update sent to `mixAudio`, undo too); `timbre-edit.test.ts` (strike partials add/edit/remove, bounds, `fm.held` fallback). e2e `sound-fx.spec.ts` (reverb decay drag + undo, Reset, delay beats + snap + Alt + Reset on Kite Day, strike partial add/edit/undo).
- **Status:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=3` 1868 passed + 4 expected fail (goldens unchanged); e2e:quick sound-fx timbre-editor voices chromium-dev/prod `--workers=1` 12/12.
## Fix: slow redraw of a short score (render, 2026-11-21)
On cb794d9. Fixes the "render perf" trap above.
- **Cause:** `cameraAt` pads are ≥ 28 px top and ≥ 54 px bottom (HUD), so below ~82 px the plot height is ≤ 0. `drawLines`/`drawPreview` set the glide tolerance to `0.35 / pxPerSemitone`, which went negative, so `sample()` subdivided every glide to the depth limit (minStep = maxStep/4096): 7.7–22.5 M path points per frame. At 82 px it was +∞ (fine); above, it shrinks with height, as intended.
- **Fix:** `chordTolerance(view)` (draw.ts, exported): `0.35 / |px per semitone|`, floored at 1e-3 semitone (also guards pHi = pLo). Normal heights unchanged: PNGs at 400 px (3 pieces × ghost frame + HUD shot × 3 times) pixel-identical.
- **Chromium draw+flush median, ms (1400 px wide, 12 s window):**

  | px | 800 | 400 | 358 | 143 | 100 | 80 | 60 |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | Kite Day before | 4.9 | 4.7 | 7.2 | 3.3 | 3.8 | 1730 | 1942 |
  | Kite Day after | 5.1 | 4.0 | 5.0 | 3.4 | 3.6 | 3.2 | 3.3 |
  | Harbour Song before | 5.2 | 4.6 | 4.4 | 6.7 | 3.7 | 1455 | 1525 |
  | Harbour Song after | 4.9 | 4.5 | 4.3 | 4.2 | 4.1 | 3.7 | 4.1 |
  | Tidewater Hymn before | 4.4 | 3.7 | 3.3 | 4.9 | 2.6 | 5664 | 5878 |
  | Tidewater Hymn after | 4.9 | 3.7 | 4.6 | 2.8 | 2.2 | 2.3 | 2.5 |

  The 143 px / 358 px gap (108 vs 69 ms) reported from the app did not reproduce in the renderer alone.
- **Open (layout, not changed):** below ~82 px the plot is mirrored (negative height between the pads), so lines draw inverted over the HUD. Shrinking the pads at small heights would fix it, but changes the score at heights the app reaches; owner/integrator call.
- **Tests:** unit `render/short-score.test.ts` (tolerance > 0 at 20–4000 px; path points at 143/82/80/60 px ≤ 400 px on all three pieces).
- **Status:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=3` 1872 passed + 4 expected fail; e2e:quick render thickness silent-lines lane-dense chromium-dev/prod `--workers=1` 29 passed, 1 skipped.
## M6-b: per-voice timbre and the `timbre` moment (core + dsp + app, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §6.3, §7.9, §8.4, §11.2, §15 M6.
- **Audit:** per-voice timbre already existed (`VoiceDef.timbre` → `CompiledVoice.timbre[0]`, flat `TIMBRE_*` records, engine A→B fade by the previous record). Missing: the moment, overlapping fades, constant power, a minimum fade.
- **Core:** `TimbreMoment { preset, fade }` (fade default 1). Simulation pushes `{t, preset, fade, moment}` per targeted voice into `VoiceRun.timbre`; `finish` builds the output timeline from `def.timbre` (read there, so a Voices-drawer timbre edit resumes incrementally), drops changes to the preset a voice is already heading to, and at one time the last change wins (at 0 it replaces the voice's own timbre). Diagnostics: `unknown-timbre` (warn, skipped; a key of `timbres` or a built-in counts), `no-targets`, `no-effect` ("every targeted note already has this sound", from `finish`). Not a glide moment (`glides()` false: no land lead-in). Incremental: timbre length in checkpoints (NUMS 22); `sameSetup` also compares `timbres` keys. Flat layout unchanged.
- **Product calls (PLAN silent):** a timbre belongs to the voice, so `'all'`, `{ voices }` and `{ part }` reach dormant voices too (a later split comes in with the new sound); `{ rank }` picks among sounding lines. A timbre change counts as activity like a level ramp (`T + fade`). No file-version bump: an older build rejects a file with timbre moments (one site, so moot).
- **Engine (`Voice.timbreMix`):** walking back from the current record, each record still fading takes sin(π/2·x) of the remaining amplitude and leaves cos to older ones, so a change mid-fade crossfades from what sounds (up to `TIMBRE_SOURCES` = 3; older are cut). With ≥ 2 sources, shares are divided by √(Σ gᵢgⱼρᵢⱼ): ρ from each slot's coherence vector (`timbre-slot.ts coherence`: table projected on sin/cos per harmonic via exact sine-table indices; FM keeps J0(nβ); additive keeps a ratio-1 partial; noise is uncorrelated power), so the power is constant whether timbres share harmonics or not. Params blend linearly. `MIN_TIMBRE_FADE` 5 ms (fade 0). One source: shares exactly 1, so render goldens are bit-identical.
- **Traps (no-GC):** a double passed to a call that is not inlined boxes (t goes through `mixT[0]`); `slotCorrelation` returned a boxed double (inlined by hand); `fade` clamp against an imported constant was a phi → `Math.max`. New no-GC mode `timbre` (24 voices changing every 1.3 s, overlapping fades, all M6 paths): 0 scavenges.
- **Numbers (tests/dsp/timbre-moment.test.ts):** wavetable/FM/noise/stretch, all 12 ordered pairs × fades 0, 10 ms, 1 s, held / mid-glide / mid-merge crossfade: worst click ratio vs the same window staying on either timbre 1.08 / 1.03 / 1.14 (held), 1.13 / 1.06 / 1.11 (gliding), 1.30 / 1.30 / 1.04 (merging); limit 2. Power through a 2 s fade within 0.69 dB of the power-interpolated ends (limit 1). Overlapping changes (3 at once) 0.39 vs 0.48 baseline; hot-swapped changes at the playhead ≤ 1.05× baseline; offline = block-by-block. Demo: `renders/m6-b/kite-day-timbre-changes.wav` (Kite Day, 5 changes; ratios 0.34–0.53 vs 0.25–0.44 without them, brighter targets).
- **App:** palette `timbre` (✧, key B; buttons 2 px narrower so 14 fit one row at 1280 px), default = a preset most voices are not on, fade 1 s; inspector Sound (select, copies a built-in into `timbres`), Fade, target via Advanced; no glide or seed. Lane: **Effects** row under Level (hidden without timbre moments): marker, preset name, fade bar; end drag edits the fade ("Change fade").
- **Perf:** RTF 15.2× plain, 11.5× M6 on a loaded machine (was 21.7 / 16.2 quiet; not re-measured quiet).
- **Tests:** unit `tests/unit/core/timbre-moment.test.ts`, `tests/unit/app/timbre-moment.test.ts`, property `timbre timelines` (arbitraries gain `timbre`); dsp `tests/dsp/timbre-moment.test.ts`, no-GC `timbre`; e2e `timbre-moment.spec.ts` (reran palette, lane*, inspector, pulse/tune UI, context, snap-beats, touch-edit, mouse-only, multi-select, copy-paste).
## Seams ahead of the playhead (render, 2026-11-21)
PLAN §11.3 "Thickness transitions" (no steps, necks, beads or end caps at the seam). Owner report: a bright knot where a dimmed rope splits.
- **Causes (measured, headless chromium, centre-line luminance):** (1) the future is drawn per shape at the ghost alpha, so where strands overlap (STRAND_LAP and shared anti-aliased edges) the alpha adds up: the bundled strands drew ~1.4× the rope's luminance for the whole crossfade window (dark 77 → 110), a bead on every merge and split in the dimmed future (and in the highlight-dimmed past). (2) Pieces meeting at a merge or split, and the opacity slices of a level fade, end in anti-aliased straight ends side by side: a 1 px hairline gap at each (up to 40 % at dpr 2). (3) The past's round cap showed past the playhead.
- **Fix (`draw.ts`):** a group is drawn straight at its alpha except over `bundleRanges` (strands.ts: the visible strand windows, padded 12 px), which go opaque into one reused offscreen canvas and are composited once at the alpha. Slices and pieces meeting at a seam are cut on device-pixel columns under a clip, the shape reaching past them (`outlinePath` `extStart`/`extEnd`), so neighbours tile exactly. Past and future are clipped at the playhead (the canvas rule's left edge, or under the DOM playhead's centre).
- **Cost:** whole-piece medians unchanged within noise. The composite is a cross-canvas sync: in rope-heavy stretches (Seven Doors 80–96 s) flush median +~1 ms; the draw-only bench loop (never presents) shows p95 spikes of 20–35 ms from it. A full-canvas layer cost 3–5 ms a frame, hence the rope regions only. `render.spec` @perf failed at load 12 (ink-24-flush 4.4 ms); not chased.
- **Known:** inside a rope region, lines that overlap without bundling (an unmerged doubling) composite as one, outside as two: a step at the region edge if one ever crosses it. Level-faded strands still overlap inside the layer (alpha < 1 there).
- **Tests:** e2e `seam.spec.ts` (13-line merge and split, light/dark, dpr 1/2, ghost 0.35/1: no bump across the seam, no cap at the playhead; level fade has no slice gaps; red on the old renderer), unit `bundleRanges`, `outlinePath` ends; `selection.test` (reveal clip found among the group clips), `strands.test` (merged lines reach 2 px past the meeting under the clip). Dev hook `__render.frame`/`bundles`, bench `range`.
- **Crops** (`/private/tmp/claude-501/artifacts/`): `{before,after}-crop-split-{dark,light}-dim-dpr2.png` (left: playhead cut ×8; right: seam ×4), `{before,after}-crop-merge-{dark,light}-dim-dpr2.png`, `{before,after}-crop-fade-dark-dpr2.png`; full frames `{before,after}-<case>.png`.
- **Follow-up (width at x ≈ 640 in the crop montages):** not a step. The `*-crop-split-*` montages join two magnifications (left ×8, right ×4 device px), so the same 12-device-px rope shows as 96 then 48 px; the widening at the right is the split's strands fanning. Measured ink per column in the full frames: 12 device px from the playhead to the split, then 13, 15, 18, 25… Single-scale strips: `{before,after}-strip-split-{dark,light}-dim-dpr2.png`. `seam.spec` now also fails on a width change of more than dpr + 1 device px between columns, up to 20 px past the seam (a split from before the playhead, a merge from its meeting: before it the arriving glides cross the measured band).

- **Engine findings** (not fixed; for the owner's list): a `shift` past a voice's range folds the line onto another of its part, which merges and dies (Switchyard's marimba before its range was widened); `tune`/`unison` on a silent part are silent no-ops at `info` level.

## Smooth master FX edits (dsp, 2026-11-21)
Follow-up to "M6 master FX and bell fields in the Sound drawer": its four `it.fails` click gaps. On cb794d9.
- **Tone** (`fx/tone.ts`): the tilt shelves' corners are fixed, so tilt only scales each high-pass output: gain and normalisation follow a per-block one-pole (τ 15 ms) and ramp per sample; the high-passes run even at tilt 0 (output untouched). Low-pass is now a TPT SVF (Butterworth, same response as the old RBJ biquad); cutoff follows in log2 Hz (τ 15 ms), coefficients every 16 samples. Bypass (≥ 98 % Nyquist) is a one-block wet/dry fade with the SVF running at that cutoff; going into bypass waits for the sweep up.
- **Reverb** (`fx/reverb.ts`): a size change crossfades each line from the old read tap to the new one (smoothstep, 50 ms; a convex blend of two delays and their RT60 gains, so the FDN stays passive); later changes wait, so a drag steps through crossfades. Target gains are computed in `setParams`. Pre-delay's read position is interpolated per sample while it moves.
- **Delay** (`fx/delay.ts`): time changes ≤ 10 ms glide (per-block one-pole, read position interpolated per sample, no more per-block jumps); larger ones crossfade old → new tap over 40 ms.
- **Clicks** (`clicks.test.ts`, now plain `it`; detector vs clean, before → after): tilt jump glass 2.07 → 0.25 (clean 0.32); tilt drag 0.56 → 0.31 (0.32, now asserted); low-pass 18 k → 600 Hz 0.61 → 0.07 (0.10); room size 0.6 → 0.15 0.36 → 0.07 (0.10); delay 1.2 → 0.375 s loud return 0.28 → 0.05 (0.06), loud send 0.47 → 0.05 (0.06). New: low-pass out of / into bypass 0.07 (0.10).
- **No-GC:** new harness mode `fx` (setMix every 0.25 s through tilt, low-pass in/out of bypass, size and delay crossfades and a glide): 0 GC, ~40 KB/min, all in `setMix`'s message handling (boxing doubles passed to `setParams`), asserted ≤ 6 KB + 256 B per edit.
- **Trap (V8):** a per-block method with no loop of its own (e.g. `Tone.process` once it delegates to helpers) runs interpreted until it tiers up, and the interpreter boxes every double it reads: the plain no-GC run went from 1.9 KB to 120 KB. Keep such entry points double-free on the steady path (boolean/int flags), pass no doubles to helpers (fields instead), and keep rarely-run smoothing inline in the hot function rather than in a helper that stays interpreted.
- **Goldens:** reverb and delay are bit-identical with constant settings; the SVF changes one sample of `fixture:stress` by one float32 LSB (1.9e-9, −174.6 dBFS); re-pinned.
- **Perf:** FX per block (µs, isolated bench) tone 1.05 → 2.2 (shelves and SVF now always run; the five tilt-0 high-passes run side by side), reverb 14.2 → 14.0, delay 0.9 → 0.8. `pnpm test:perf` on the cloud box: RTF 10.6× (24 voices + FX) and 5.5× M6 (gate ≥ 10×, fails at baseline here: 6.7× before, 6.1× after in a back-to-back run; noise-level). No-GC: all runs 1864 B, `fx` 17–40 KB.
- **Status:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=3` 1876 passed (was 1868 + 4 expected fail).

## Score minimum height (app layout + render, 2026-11-21)
On cf65a97. Fixes the "Open (layout)" item above: with a drawer open on a ≤ 768 px window the score got ~80 px (0 at 1024×600) and drew inverted.
- **Layout (styles.css):** `--score-min: min(204px, 34vh)` on `.stage` (canvas 202, plot 120 px), `--lane-min: 44px` (= `SPLIT_MIN_LANE`). The workspace is `flex: 1 1 0` with `min-height` = ruler + score + divider + lanes + gap + `--viewbar-h` (ViewBar.tsx measures it: it wraps), and the timeline row is `minmax(--timeline-min, 1fr)`. Drawers are `flex: 0 1 auto`, `min-height: min(80px, 12vh)`; their bodies scroll.
- **Order (deviation from the brief):** the lanes give way first (down to 44 px), then the open drawer, then the page scrolls. Drawer-first would need the workspace minimum to include the lanes' height, which is a share of the timeline (circular).
- **Trap: the view bar is 350 px tall at 1024 px wide** (Palette, Suggestions, 4 control rows, keys hint; 269 px at 1280). So 1024×600 and 1024×768 can't hold transport + 204 px score + view bar + a drawer: the page scrolls by ~80–250 px with a drawer open. Compacting the view bar is the real fix (integrator call).
- **Divider:** `splitLanePx`/`clampLanePx` take `scoreMin` (the stage's computed min-height, read in Lane.tsx), so a stored split never takes the score under it. At 1024×768 the default lanes are now ~50 px (score 204; was 188 / 113).
- **Render (camera.ts `scorePads`):** when the 28/54 pads exceed 60 % of the canvas (< ~137 px with the HUD), both shrink proportionally, so the plot keeps ≥ 40 % and is never inverted. `hudLines(padB)`: the HUD keeps the first lines that fit, bottom-anchored. Unchanged at ≥ 137 px.
- **Tests:** unit short-score (plot > 0 and ≥ 40 % at 20–4000 px, with and without HUD; HUD lines inside the pad), lane.test (split vs `scoreMin`); e2e `score-min.spec` (1024×600, 1280×720 × Sound Master / Notes: canvas ≥ min, lanes ≥ 44, drawer below the workspace and scrolling, voice pitches inside the upright plot; red before: canvas 0). `lane-dense` divider test moved to 1280×800 with a 40 px drag (no room at 1024×768 now).
- **Status:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=3` 1875 passed + 4 expected fail; e2e:quick lane-dense lane-rows sound-fx timbre-editor voices render score-min chromium-dev/prod `--workers=1` 49 passed, 1 skipped.
## Tune: Melody on moves the tune; option hover highlights the line (app + render, 2026-11-21)
Owner: "changing what note a tune is on should move the tune to that melody"; "hovering over a note in the menu should highlight that line". On 405bf4f.
- **Move** (`editor/tune.ts`): `melodyMove` = `melodyPatch` + the notes shifted from the line playing now (`tuneLineAt`, its pitch before the tune's first move) to the line that plays after the pick (the pick compiled, so Highest/Lowest land where the compiler puts them). `shiftMelody`: both line pitches in the key at `at` → every note moves that many scale steps (key at each note's start), an accidental or cents keeping its offset from the scale tone below; a line off the key → semitones. A note outside the new line's range folds by octaves. Same line or same pitch: no `notes` in the patch. Still one undo entry; the audition plays the moved first note.
- **Listbox** (`TuneFields.tsx MelodyOn`): the native select is now a button (`insp-melody-on`, `data-value` = choice, `aria-haspopup=listbox`) + popup `ul[role=listbox]` (`insp-melody-list`, options `insp-melody-option` with `data-value`, `aria-activedescendant`). ↓/↑ on the button opens; in the list ↑/↓/Home/End, Enter picks, Escape closes (preventDefault, so the selection stays), Tab/blur/outside click close. No type-ahead.
- **Highlight:** pointer or arrow on an option → `setLineHighlight(melodyVoice(...))` (`app/line-highlight.ts`); the loop then passes `highlightVoices` (new `ScoreStyle` field, render) instead of the selection's `highlight`, drawing that whole line thick with the rest dimmed (same path as the selection, plus its silent trace). Cleared on pointer leave, close, unmount. `frameStats.highlightVoice` (debug hook) reports the drawn line.
- **Product calls:** (1) the whole line is highlighted, not just the tune's span; (2) while an option is pointed at, it replaces the selection highlight (else the tune's own line stays lit and the two are hard to tell apart); (3) the shift is relative: the first note keeps its interval to the line (a tune an octave above C3 lands an octave above E4).
- **Tests:** unit `tune-melody.test.ts` (scale steps up/down, accidental and cents offsets, off-key semitones, range folding, same-pitch no-op, note/Highest/Lowest moves, same-line no-op, compiled first note on the new line); `render/selection.test.ts` (`highlightVoices`). e2e `tune-ui` "Melody on" (pick E4 → notes move 9 steps, Highest by keyboard, undo ×2 restores notes) and "option … highlights its line" (open, hover, Highest, leave, arrows, Escape).
- **Status:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=3` 1898 passed + 4 expected fail. e2e:quick tune-ui tune-dots inspector chromium-dev/prod `--workers=1` 60/60. Seen once in repeats, not from this change: "Random melody" (tune-ui:280) failed 1 of 6 on prod; another builder's e2e on the box also took down the dev server once (connection refused).

On 759d92b. Fixes the "Score minimum height" trap: the bar under the lanes was 350 / 269 / 225 px tall at 1024 / 1280 / 1440 wide; now 116 / 74 / 74 (kite-day; 68 at 390). With the Notes drawer open the page no longer scrolls at 1024×768 (was +68 px).

Row 2 (`.vb-row`): Zoom (−, slider `clamp(110px, 14vw, 220px)`, +, value, Fit; the "Zoom" word dropped, group name kept), Follow, Snap to beats, then Notes, Sound, "View ▾", "?" at the right. 1280/1440: 2 rows; 1024: 3 (Next on its own row).

- **Tests:** new `viewbar.spec` (height ≤ 100 at 1280×800, ≤ 140 at 1024×768, no page scroll with Notes open; Add + Next share a row, none clipped at 1280; View popover by keyboard, theme + HUD, Escape, outside press; "?"; 390 px no horizontal scroll). Specs updated to open View first: `app` (colours, themes, screenshots), `dots` (keep order). `palette`: Next beside or below Add. `lane-dense`: the dense lanes now fit at 1280×720/800, so the scroll-into-view test shrinks the window by 160 px and the divider test runs at 1024×700. `touch-edit` octave test: the "empty score" long press moved from 10 % to 75 % height (the taller score put a line at 10 %).
- **Status:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=3` 1903 passed; e2e:quick viewbar view-follow palette suggestions snap-beats lane-dense score-min app mouse-only editor-polish dots touch-edit chromium-dev/prod 167 passed, 1 skipped; `playwright test mouse-only` chromium 2 passed; 20 more editor specs chromium-dev 118 passed.

## Root in the bass; Roman numeral hint (core + app, 2026-11-22)
composing.md §3 suggestions #2 and #10. On 7781272.
- **#2 core** (`moments/chord.ts bassLine`): a `chord` NoteSet on exactly one logical voice (a one-line part, `{ voices: [x] }`, a rank of size 1), with no count change, goes to the slash bass, else the root: the octave nearest its committed destination (ties down) within its range, and within `register` if that holds one; not onto a pitch another line of its part holds (nearest free octave; none → usual voicing). Every voicing mode. Not applied: `pitches`/`degrees`/`current` NoteSets, `byVoice` (returns earlier), from silence (establish path), a chord `count` that adds or removes lines, a merged line of count > 1 (it is several logical voices). Per-voice overrides still win (applied after the plan). Before: k = 1 took the chord tone nearest the register's middle (slash: the bass nearest the middle).
- **Pieces affected:** none. All 5 examples and 8 compositions compile identically before and after (segment-by-segment compare); no chord symbol in them targets one line (basses are tunes). Render goldens, generate and compose-steps snapshots unchanged.
- **#10 app** (`inspector/fields.ts numeralHint`, `NoteSetField.tsx NumeralHintRow`): under the chord Symbol field, for the committed symbol. Minor-type = the scale has a minor 3rd and no major 3rd (aeolian, dorian, phrygian, locrian, harmonic/melodic minor, minor pentatonic, blues, minor-3rd custom scales). Shown when the symbol starts with an unaltered numeral whose root is outside the scale and whose flat is in it; not for applied chords (`V/V`). Notes are spelled on the numeral's letter (VI in D minor: B / B♭). "Use ♭VI" rewrites to `b` + symbol (case, suffix and slash kept), one undo entry "Flatten numeral", and auditions.
- **Tests:** unit `core/root-in-bass.test.ts`, `app/numeral-hint.test.ts`; e2e `numeral-hint.spec.ts`.
## Bounded number fields get sliders (app UI, 2026-11-21)
Owner: "split should have a slider for notes after, not a box to type in. Can this be a general thing for bounded numerical inputs?" On 7781272.
- **Rule** (`inspector/fields.ts sliderPlan`, used by `NumberField`): an explicit `slider: {min,max}` wins, `slider={false}` means box only; otherwise a field gets a slider over its typed range when that range is finite and spans ≤ 101 integers (`SLIDER_MAX_INTS`; 0–100 % fits, pitches 0–127 don't) or ≤ 1000 steps (`SLIDER_MAX_STEPS`). Integer sliders step by whole numbers; ≤ 16 values get a tick per value (`<datalist>`). An integer slider covering the whole typed range with ≤ 32 values is a **count**: slider plus the value as a label (`${testId}-value`), no text box. Everything else keeps its compact box beside the slider. Slider test id is always `${testId}-slider`. min = max (a pool of 1) shows a disabled slider.
- **Gained sliders:** split and merge "Notes after" (counts, live bounds), chord "Notes" (count; was slider + box), walk "Leap min/max" (now counts, box dropped), target "Lowest/Highest rank" (counts), Bars "Beats per bar" (count), Bars "Tempo" (20–300, with box), spread "Factor" (0–8, with box), merge "Fade" (slider 0–10 s, typed to 60, like timbre fade), Bézier x1/y1/x2/y2 (with box; the Bézier grid is one column now). Already had one: walk region/grid/step glide/density, pulse region/step/decay/floor, glide length/stagger, note "Return after", level ramp, timbre fade.
- **Box only, by judgement:** moment Time (unbounded), every pitch field (`pitchProps.slider = false`: note names are typed; 128 values too fine), WeightBox / tune note lengths (compact list-row boxes, no room). Notes drawer: pitch ranges (pitches) and Pool size (a drag would sweep through adding/removing notes and the moments that act only on them); the Sound drawer already pairs sliders with boxes.
- **Live bounds:** split count ∈ [lines sounding just before it (`soundingBefore`, lines born at `at` not counted), pool size], so births ≤ pool − sounding (count is the total after the split, §6.2). Merge count ∈ [1, sounding before]. Both widen to hold a stored count outside that range. Chord count ∈ [1, pool].
- **Drags:** `fieldSetter` drags (sliders and label scrubbing) now coalesce to one piece update per animation frame (as `timbre-edit` drags); `end` flushes the last frame, cancel drops it. One gesture = one undo entry; each arrow-key press on a slider is one entry. No new auditions.
- **Tests:** unit `bounded-sliders.test.ts` (plan decisions, split/merge bounds, `soundingBefore`). E2E `bounded-sliders.spec` (split slider drag 5 → 12: compiled lines after the split follow, one undo; merge count by arrows, one entry each; merge fade slider + box). Updated: `inspector.spec` (chord Notes and Leap min by slider), `snap.spec` (Beats per bar by arrow).
- **Status:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=2` 1930 passed; e2e:quick of the 26 `insp-` specs chromium-dev/prod `--workers=1` 242 passed; `playwright test mouse-only` chromium 2 passed.
## M6 acceptance audit (tests + docs, 2026-11-22)
On 7781272. Report: `docs/m6-acceptance.md`. Every §15 M6 line passes; no product code changed. lint and typecheck clean; `pnpm test --maxWorkers=2` 1949 passed, 1 skipped; e2e:quick timbre-editor timbre-moment voices sound-fx chromium-dev 9/9.
- **New `tests/dsp/preset-switch.test.ts`:** preset changes during playback through `AudioEngine.swap`'s sequence: tables whose definition changed, then `Graph.swap`, on a compiled piece. Three paths cover all 210 ordered pairs of the 12 built-ins plus FM 1.41, FM 2.76 and `M6A_BELL`: Voices drawer `setAllTimbres`, a same-name redefinition, and a fade-0 `timbre` moment 50 ms behind the playhead. The worst results are 51 / 48 / 52 % of the limit (2× the cleaner timbre); a hard-switch control scores a median 128 %. It also covers Sound drawer drags (one table set per frame), edits 10 ms after a strike, reinstalls inside the 20 ms crossfade, and save → load → bit-identical render (preset file and piece file).
- **New `tests/dsp/fx.test.ts`** (§8.6, each effect alone): reverb RT60, damping, pre-delay, determinism and L/R decorrelation; ping-pong delay timing and feedback; tone low-pass −3 dB at the cutoff and tilt per octave; the limiter's 5 ms look-ahead.
- **Finding (not fixed):** the reverb's broadband RT60 is 10–13 % short of its setting at damping 0 (set 1 / 3 s: 0.895 / 2.632 s; under 200 Hz 1.024 / 3.019 s). The cause is linear interpolation of the fractional line reads. The test pins 0.85–1.05×.
- **Seen in code:** after more than 64 timbre names in one session, `Graph.recycleSlot` can free a slot whose name `AudioEngine.sent` still marks as sent, so its tables are never resent.

## Reverb RT60 and timbre slot bookkeeping (dsp + audio, 2026-11-22)
Fixes the two findings of "M6 acceptance audit". On 7913e16.
- **Reverb** (`fx/reverb.ts`): line lengths are whole samples (`Math.round`, `Int32Array`); reads are plain taps, no interpolation. Gains still come from the (now integer) lengths. The size crossfade is unchanged, now between integer taps. Damping 0, broadband RT60 before → after: set 1 s 0.895 → 0.995 s, set 3 s 2.632 → 2.993 s; size 0 / 1 within 0.5 %; < 200 Hz unchanged (1.024 / 3.024 s). `fx.test.ts` asserts ±3 % (broadband and < 200 Hz) at sizes 0, 0.6, 1. Damping keeps its effect (sizes 0.6 / 0.75, decay 1 / 3.2 s): the high-band tail (2nd difference) at damping 0.45–0.8 moves −2…+10 % (e.g. 3.2 s, 0.45: 0.646 → 0.636 s); at 0.25 it is 5–19 % longer; at 0 it now matches the setting (was 48–73 % of it).
- **Goldens:** all 30 re-pinned on Linux (every fixture and piece has reverb). Every piece uses damping 0.4–0.55, where the damping filter dominates, so the change is small: reverb return (FX stem, delay off, no limiter) +0.0 to +0.43 dB (pieces −0.04…+0.21, fixtures +0.01…+0.43), tail after the end (EDC, −5…−35 dB) +0.00…+0.11 s (largest: little-round 3.73 → 3.84, night-ferry 4.30 → 4.37). Listening pair: `renders/reverb-rt60/tidewater-hymn-0-40s-{before,after}.m4a` (AAC 192k, not committed).
- **Timbre slots:** `AudioEngine.sent` is now exactly the set of names the worklet holds, in LRU order. Before posting a score whose names would take it past `MAX_TIMBRE_SLOTS`, `makeRoom` evicts the oldest names not used by the new score, the worklet's current score or the last real score, and posts `{ type: 'forget', timbres }` (new protocol message) before anything else, so a name that comes back has its tables rebuilt and resent. `Graph.forget` frees the slot's name but keeps its tables until reuse (fading voices keep sounding); `slotIndex` prefers free slots no voice references. `recycleSlot` stays as a last resort (one score with > 64 names), unreported. Also: `audition` now posts the tables it marked sent even when it refuses (it used to drop them, leaving `sent` wrong).
- **Tests:** new `tests/dsp/timbre-slots.test.ts`: the real `AudioEngine` with a fake worklet node feeding a `Graph`; 70 names in turn, then back to t0: forgets t0…t5 in order, never recycles, resends t0's tables, and t0 renders as itself (7th-harmonic ratio within 10 % of a fresh render, level within 1 dB). Without `makeRoom` it fails (no tables resent). Plus `Graph.forget` keeping a playing voice's tables.
- **Trap:** a name whose table build is still in flight when it is evicted would leave the worklet one slot over; it needs 64 newer names in between, so it is not handled.
- **Status:** lint, typecheck, `pnpm test --maxWorkers=2` 1976 passed, 1 skipped (+ fx size cases since, 9/9). `pnpm test:perf`: RTF M6 6.7× (gate ≥ 10×, fails at baseline here); no-GC 0 GC lines in every mode, but the `fx` mode's byte budget (≤ 67 KB) fails at 137–163 KB both on this branch and on plain 7913e16 on this box (not from this change; 17–40 KB when it was set).
